Each category is associated with structured cytomorphology prompts that describe diagnostic morphological attributes and provide clinically grounded textual knowledge. We further propose CytoSPM, an efficient detector based on a decoupled two-stage design. It first extracts reusable class-agnostic visual representations, and then performs class-aware structural prompt matching with class names and cytomorphology prompts. On PentaCyto, CytoSPM outperforms existing methods in novel-category detection and open-vocabulary detection while maintaining efficient inference.

</details>


### [69] [CG-HAF: An Interpretable Global-Local Lesion-Burden Fusion Framework for Ordinal Acne Severity Grading in Agentic Skincare Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31326)
*Muhammad Muhtasim Shahriar,Md. Naimur Asif Borno,Saad Aloteibi,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CG-HAF全局-局部融合框架用于有序痤疮严重度分级：将独立训练的全局分类器输出概率与目标检测器提取的病灶负担描述符（数量、置信度、面积）显式结合，再用轻量可解释分类器输出最终等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序痤疮分级需区分视觉上相邻的相似等级，同时兼顾整体面部外观与局部病灶分布；现有方法多将两类证据压缩为单一不透明表示，缺乏可解释性与跨标准泛化能力。

Method: CG-HAF保持证据显式分离：对全局严重度概率做平均，与检测器给出的结构化病灶负担描述符拼接成紧凑表示，输入轻量可解释分类器进行有序分级；并设计诊断实验分析跨数据集性能差距的来源。

Result: 在常用基准上，该融合相较仅用全局证据的基线取得统计显著提升，最严重病例增益最大；在采用不同分级标准的独立数据集上，强域内性能无法自动迁移，诊断表明差距主要来自分级标准不匹配而非检测失败。

Conclusion: 可解释的全局-局部融合是有效的有序痤疮分级策略；分级标准对齐是跨数据集可移植性的关键，该严重度信号可支持护肤应用中透明、非诊断性的决策。

Abstract: Ordinal acne severity grading requires distinguishing visually similar neighboring grades while jointly weighing holistic facial appearance and localized lesion burden - evidence that most existing approaches collapse into a single opaque representation. We introduce CG-HAF, a global-local fusion framework that instead keeps this evidence explicit: averaged holistic severity probabilities from independently trained classifiers are combined with structured lesion-burden descriptors from an object detector (lesion count, detection confidence, lesion area) into a compact representation, from which a lightweight, interpretable classifier produces the final grade. On a widely used benchmark, this fusion yields a clear, statistically supported improvement over global-evidence-only baselines, with the largest gains on the most severe cases. Testing on an independent dataset with a different grading standard shows that strong within-dataset performance does not transfer automatically, and a follow-up diagnostic attributes much of this gap to mismatched grading criteria rather than detection failure alone. These findings support interpretable global-local fusion as an effective strategy for ordinal acne grading while highlighting criterion alignment as key to cross-dataset portability, with a further illustration of how the resulting severity signal can support transparent, non-diagnostic decision-making in skincare applications.

</details>


### [70] [DyMD: Preserving Interaction Dynamics through Distribution Matching Distillation in Few-Step Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31349)
*Haojun Xu,Jie Huang,Xin Lu,Mingchen Zhong,Zihao Fan,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DyMD，通过时间亲和度条件化重噪采样与动力学引导的伪分数跟踪，自适应调整教师监督与判别器拟合，将 14B 视频扩散教师蒸馏为四步 1.3B 学生模型，在具身视频生成与下游动作规划上显著优于 Base DMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视频扩散模型作为具身预测先验表达力强，但多步采样代价高，难以支持交互式下游使用。现有 Distribution Matching Distillation（DMD）虽能少步生成视频，却会抑制机器人-物体运动，在保持视觉质量的同时丢失交互动力学，影响下游动作规划。

Method: 作者诊断 DMD 的教师与伪分数信号：弱重噪使教师后验集中在运动贫乏的 rollout 附近，限制恢复运动的引导；而强运动 rollout 往往带来更大的伪分数拟合误差，妨碍生成器学习交互动力学。为此提出 DyMD：1）时间亲和度条件化重噪采样，将基础时间步调度与由局部后验变化动机得到的教师先验混合，按每条 rollout 当前交互保真度自适应时间步分布；2）动力学引导伪分数跟踪，用噪声条件预测器从潜在时序动力学估计噪声相对的拟合难度，在判别器损失中对预测为难样本的 rollout 加权。

Result: 将 14B 教师蒸馏为 1.3B 四步学生模型，推理时无需任何辅助模块。在具身视频基准上，相对 Base DMD：R-Bench 任务遵循度提升 9.6 个百分点，PAI-Bench-G 域得分提升 5.1 分，视觉质量相当。作为下游动作规划骨干，在两项 WorldArena 任务上平均成功率 34%，Base DMD 为 16%。

Conclusion: DyMD 表明少步视频扩散蒸馏中的运动退化可通过同时自适应教师监督与判别器拟合来缓解，兼得运动恢复与外观精修，并可作为具身策略/动作规划的高效生成式骨干。

Abstract: Large video diffusion models offer expressive priors for embodied prediction and learning, yet their many-step sampling remains costly for interactive downstream use. Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) enables few-step video generation, but can suppress robot--object motion while preserving visual quality. Examining DMD's teacher and fake-score signals, we find that weak re-noising keeps the teacher posterior concentrated near motion-deficient rollouts, limiting motion-restoring guidance. Meanwhile, stronger-motion rollouts tend to incur larger fake-score fitting errors, which can hinder the generator's learning of interaction dynamics. We propose DyMD, a DMD framework that adapts both teacher supervision and critic fitting to the evolving student. Temporal affinity--conditioned re-noise sampling adapts the timestep distribution to each rollout's current interaction fidelity by mixing the base schedule with a teacher prior motivated by local posterior variation, thereby balancing motion recovery and appearance refinement. To better track stronger-motion rollouts, dynamics-guided fake-score tracking uses a noise-conditioned predictor to estimate noise-relative fitting difficulty from latent temporal dynamics, then upweights predicted-hard rollouts in the critic loss. Using DyMD, we distill a 14B teacher into a four-step 1.3B student with no auxiliary modules at inference. On embodied-video benchmarks, the student improves R-Bench task adherence by $9.6$ percentage points and PAI-Bench-G Domain score by $5.1$ points over Base DMD while maintaining comparable visual quality. As a backbone for downstream action planning, our student achieves 34% mean success across two WorldArena tasks, compared with 16% for Base DMD.

</details>


### [71] [Open Vocabulary Domain Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31356)
*Sumanth Udupa,Mehrtash Harandi,Yadan Luo,Mahsa Baktashmotlagh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出近似域遗忘（ADU）存在闭词表缺陷，提出开放词表域遗忘（OVDU）协议，并给出基于Fisher信息掩码与目标流形散射的参数编辑框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM零样本泛化强，但会编码理想化教材图、卡通车等有害风格域。已有ADU方法仅在遗忘微调中见过的类上评估，导致只是过拟合类-域对，对新类仍可识别该域，产生"已删除"的假象。作者主张真正的域擦除必须与类无关。

Method: 分两步：(1) Fisher信息掩码定位对域敏感的权重，从数学上保护基础零样本泛化能力；(2) 目标流形散射（TMS）目标，通过偏好挖掘在局部打散遗忘域的style几何结构。

Result: 在PACS、OfficeHome、DomainNet上，开放词表泛化显著优于现有基线；样本效率极高，仅用4 shots即超过基线8-shot峰值。

Conclusion: 提出并形式化了OVDU协议，强调域遗忘必须迁移到留出类，参数编辑框架在保持泛化的同时实现类无关域擦除。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit remarkable zero-shot generalization, yet they often encode unwanted or hazardous stylistic domains such as idealized textbook diagrams in medical AI or cartoon vehicles in autonomous driving. Approximate Domain Unlearning (ADU) aims to selectively erase a model's recognition of a target visual domain while preserving accuracy on the remaining domains. However, existing ADU methods operate under a flawed closed-vocabulary assumption: they evaluate unlearning solely on the specific object classes seen during the unlearning fine-tuning phase. Consequently, these methods do not unlearn the domain itself; they merely overfit to seen class-domain pairs, leaving the domain easily recognizable for unseen classes and providing a false sense of removal. We argue that true domain erasure must be class-agnostic. To address this, we formalize Open-Vocabulary Domain Unlearning (OVDU), a rigorous protocol that mandates domain forgetting must transfer to held-out classes. To solve the OVDU challenge, we propose a surgical parameter-editing framework. First, a Fisher Information mask isolates domain-sensitive weights, mathematically protecting foundational zero-shot generalization. Second, our Targeted Manifold Scattering (TMS) objective uses preference-based mining to locally scatter the forget domain's stylistic geometry. Evaluated across PACS, OfficeHome, and DomainNet, our method vastly improves open-vocabulary generalization over existing baselines. Crucially, it delivers exceptional sample efficiency, outperforming peak 8-shot baseline results with only 4 shots.

</details>


### [72] [OpenVAM: Open-World Visual Attention Modeling with VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31364)
*Kiana Hooshanfar,Amirhossein Kazerouni,Alireza Hosseini,Michael Brudno,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenVAM 提出统一框架，通过“密集视觉通路 + 指令跟随 VLM 语义头”的解耦对齐设计，同时实现开放世界注视点定位与 what/why 解释，并用三阶段训练与多域标注流水线提升跨域鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉注意力建模多只输出稠密显著图，无法把注意力峰值与场景中的离散元素（what）及峰值的成因（why）关联起来，难以支撑设计分析、机器人和 HCI 等实际动作决策；同时跨自然图像、商业内容、UI/网页布局存在显著域偏移，缺乏同时兼顾通用性与可解释性的方法。

Method: OpenVAM 采用解耦但对齐的双通路结构：一条专用稠密视觉通路负责稳定、空间精确的显著点定位；一个指令跟随的视觉-语言语义头在同一图像与数据类型提示条件下生成有依据的 what/why 解释。配合三阶段训练策略，在保持强定位先验的同时逐步引入语言 grounding，并通过参数高效微调改进解释对齐而不扰动显著分支；此外提出可扩展流水线，生成多域显著-理由标注用于训练与系统评测。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，OpenVAM 在域偏移下具有更好的鲁棒性，并能产生与图像内容接地的解释，使显著预测更具可解释性。摘要未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: OpenVAM 表明通用性与可解释性可以联合建模：解耦对齐的双通路设计与分阶段训练在跨域显著预测和 grounded 解释上均有效，为面向实际动作的注意力建模提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Predicting human gaze is a core capability for applications ranging from web/UI design analysis to robotics and human-computer interaction. Yet, most visual attention modeling methods output only a dense saliency map, which is often insufficient for action: practitioners need to connect attention peaks to discrete elements in the scene (what) and understand the drivers of those peaks in context (why), while remaining robust to domain shift across natural images, commercial content, and UI/web layouts. We, therefore, introduce OpenVAM (Open-world Visual Attention Modeling with VLMs), a unified framework that jointly addresses universality and explainability across heterogeneous domains (natural scenes, commercial imagery, and UI/web layouts) and supervision modalities. OpenVAM adopts a decoupled-but-aligned design: a dedicated dense visual pathway provides stable, spatially precise localization, while an instruction-following vision--language semantic head generates grounded what/why explanations conditioned on the same image and data-type prompts. A three-stage training strategy preserves strong localization priors while progressively introducing language grounding and improving explanation alignment via parameter-efficient adaptation without perturbing the saliency branch. We further propose a scalable pipeline to generate multi-domain saliency-reason annotations for training and systematic evaluation. Experiments across diverse datasets show that OpenVAM improves robustness under domain shift while producing image-grounded explanations that make saliency predictions more interpretable.

</details>


### [73] [ContraFM-S2O: Flow Matching-Based One-step SAR-to-Optical Image Translation Model with Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31378)
*Mingqian Yu,Wei-kuan Chiang,Qiurui Wang,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ContraFM-S2O，一种基于流匹配的SAR到光学图像翻译模型，通过预测速度场、推理时求解ODE并采用平均速度实现单步生成，结合对比学习提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAR-to-optical翻译主流方法（扩散模型与GAN）存在推理延迟高、生成图像细节保真度低（边缘模糊、纹理丢失）的问题，需兼顾采样效率与生成质量。

Method: 基于流匹配框架，训练时学习预测速度场，推理时求解ODE而非SDE以提升采样效率；用插值路径上的平均速度替代瞬时速度，实现单步SAR-to-optical翻译；引入对比学习提升生成光学图像质量。

Result: 在SAR2Opt和QXS数据集上达到state-of-the-art，超越基线方法，并通过单步生成降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 流匹配结合平均速度单步生成与对比学习，可在SAR-to-optical翻译中同时提升质量与效率，优于扩散与GAN类方法。

Abstract: In recent years, diffusion models and GAN-based models have become the mainstream approaches for SAR-to-optical image translation, owing to their advantages, such as high-quality generation and stable training. However, they have shortcomings such as high inference latency and the generated optical images suffer from low detail fidelity, often resulting in blurred edges and loss of fine textures. Thus, we propose ContraFM-S2O, which is a flow matching-based model for SAR-to-optical image translation. Unlike conventional diffusion models, ContraFM-S2O learns to predict the velocity field in training and solves ODE instead of SDE during inference to improve the sampling efficiency. In addition, ContraFM-S2O replaces instantaneous velocity with average velocity along the interpolation path to realize one-step SAR-to-optical image translation and uses contrastive learning to improve the quality of the generated optical images. Experiments show our model achieves state-of-the-art on SAR2Opt and QXS datasets, outperforming baselines, and reduces inference latency via one-step generation.

</details>


### [74] [AxonSynth: Domain-Randomized Synthetic Data for Zero-Shot 3D Axon Segmentation in Light-Sheet Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31431)
*Edward Gaibor,Kyriaki-Margarita Bintsi,Carmen Luz Leiva Ureta,Zayneb Bellatif,Chiara Maffei,Wenze Li,Elizabeth Hillman,Yaël Balbastre,Anastasia Yendiki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AxonSynth，一个域随机化合成数据框架，用合成轴突标签训练 3D U-Net，实现无需真实标注的跨样本零样本 3D 轴突分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 显微数据中轴突的精确分割对白质结构分析至关重要，但稠密真实标注成本高昂；现有监督方法依赖目标域标注，在组织类型、物种、模态或采集条件变化时性能脆弱。

Method: AxonSynth 生成带方向先验的稠密合成轴突标签以反映真实纤维构型，并随机化密度、对比度、偏置场、模糊与噪声进行渲染；训练三分类 3D U-Net 预测背景、轴突鞘与轴突内空间；在 10 个来自猕猴与人脑、含三种轴突标记的光片显微（LSM）留出块上做零样本迁移评估，与校准阈值化和 Frangi 滤波对比，采用重叠度、校正检测、假阳性与拓扑指标。

Result: 猕猴样本上 AxonSynth 取得最佳校正 Dice 与校正精度（0.826 / 0.851），优于阈值化（0.765 / 0.754）和 Frangi（0.685 / 0.762）；人类样本上校正 Dice 与阈值化相当（0.857 vs. 0.868），但连通分量计数误差从 22,504 降至 3,377；全部 10 个留出块中 10/10 降低分量计数误差，8/10 降低欧拉示性数误差。

Conclusion: 合成标签域随机化可减少对手工轴突标注的依赖，支持合成到真实的 3D 分割迁移。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of axons in 3D microscopy data is important for analyzing white-matter organization, but dense ground truth labels are expensive to obtain. Existing supervised axon segmentation methods rely on target-domain annotations and can be brittle when tissue type, species, modality, or acquisition conditions change. We present AxonSynth, a domain-randomized synthetic-data framework for training 3D axon segmentation models without manually annotated real training volumes. AxonSynth generates dense synthetic axon labels with orientation priors that reflect realistic fiber configurations and renders them with randomized density, contrast, bias fields, blur, and noise. A three-class 3D U-Net is trained to predict background, axon sheath and intra-axonal space. We evaluate zero-shot transfer on 10 held-out light-sheet microscopy (LSM) patches from macaque and human brain samples labeled with one of three axonal markers, comparing against calibrated thresholding and Frangi filtering using overlap, corrected detection, false-positive, and topology metrics. On macaque samples, AxonSynth achieved the best corrected Dice and corrected precision (0.826 and 0.851), compared with 0.765 and 0.754 for thresholding and 0.685 and 0.762 for Frangi. On human samples, corrected Dice was comparable to thresholding (0.857 vs. 0.868), while component-count error decreased from 22,504 to 3,377. Across all held-out patches, AxonSynth reduced component-count error in 10/10 patches and Euler-characteristic error in 8/10. These results show that synthetic-label domain randomization can reduce dependence on manual axon annotation while supporting synthetic-to-real 3D segmentation.

</details>


### [75] [Implicit Neural Representation for Hyperspectral Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31435)
*Alfredo Scalera,Paul Murray,Jaime Zabalza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作将隐式神经表示(INR)扩展到高光谱视频压缩,相比逐帧传统高光谱图像压缩取得+4.99 dB BD-PSNR与-88.88% BD-rate增益,并在HOT2026数据集上提升下游目标跟踪性能(跟踪AUC最高+23.42%,距离精度最高+35.56%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快照式相机普及使高光谱视频数据日益易得,数据集规模不断增大,但高光谱视频压缩仍处于早期阶段,缺乏高效压缩方案;同时需要验证压缩对下游任务(如目标跟踪)的影响。

Method: 以隐式神经表示作为压缩载体,在已有RGB视频压缩模型基础上提出扩展,使其适配高光谱视频的高维光谱特性,构建面向高光谱视频的INR压缩框架,并与PCA+JPEG2000等传统逐帧方案在低数据量条件下对比。

Result: 相比逐帧传统高光谱图像压缩,BD-PSNR提升+4.99 dB,BD-rate降低88.88%;在HOT2026数据集的低数据率场景下,相比PCA与JPEG2000压缩视频,跟踪AUC最高提升23.42%,距离精度最高提升35.56%。

Conclusion: 隐式神经表示是高光谱视频压缩的有效方案,不仅在重建质量上显著优于传统方法,还能更好地保留对下游跟踪任务有用的信息,为高光谱视频的高效存储与传输提供了新思路。

Abstract: With the advent of snapshot cameras, hyperspectral video is becoming more readily available. In recent years, new applications have emerged which have led to increasingly larger datasets. However, hyperspectral video compression remains in the early stages. In this study, we explore the use of implicit neural representation as a candidate solution. We propose a novel extension of an existing RGB video compression model, achieving Bjøntegaard Delta PSNR gains of +4.99 dB and Bjøntegaard Delta rate of -88.88% compared to traditional hyperspectral image compression methods applied frame-by-frame. In addition to reconstruction quality, the effects on downstream task performance are measured in the form of object tracking success. Compared to video compressed with methods based on principal component analysis and JPEG2000 in low data regimes, our proposed method improves tracking area under the curve by up to 23.42% and distance precision by up to 35.56% on examples from the HOT2026 dataset.

</details>


### [76] [From Reward Signal to Visual Utility: A Controlled Audit of Medical VLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31450)
*Wang Jingxin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文对医疗VLM（Qwen2.5-VL-3B + PMC-VQA）的后训练进行受控测量审计，发现SFT、LoRA范围扩展、标准GRPO与反事实证据目标的优化活动与答案准确率、图像条件决策之间的脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗视觉语言模型后训练通常仅用答案准确率评估，无法反映模型是否真正依赖图像进行决策。作者希望厘清"优化活动、目标习得、有用的留出视觉行为"三者之间的关系。

Method: 在PMC-VQA上对Qwen2.5-VL-3B进行受控实验：对比仅语言模型LoRA的SFT、扩大多模态适应范围、纯答案GRPO、以及反事实证据目标；在2000题干净测试集上做配对bootstrap置信区间、视觉增益事件与图像敏感度分析；并审计生成路径上的token评分与答案生成路径差异。

Result: 语言模型LoRA SFT使正确图像准确率+1.10pp（95% CI: -0.85~+3.05，不显著），但视觉增益事件-2.40pp、图像敏感度-5.60pp；配对记录显示155个获得与203个丢失的视觉增益事件。更广适应范围准确率更低；标准GRPO产生混合奖励组与参数更新，干净测试变化不确定；评分沿贪心生成路径时证据目标在训练集上改善，但在验证集上相对GRPO的增益不一致。

Conclusion: 准确率提升不能等价于模型更有效地利用图像；优化活动、目标习得与留出视觉行为之间存在系统性缺口，需引入图像条件决策层面的评估指标。

Abstract: Medical vision-language model (VLM) post-training is commonly evaluated through answer accuracy. We examine how changes in accuracy and training objectives relate to image-conditioned decisions in a controlled Qwen2.5-VL-3B study on PMC-VQA. We compare supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with low-rank adaptation (LoRA) restricted to the language model, expanded multimodal adaptation scopes, standard answer-only Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), and a counterfactual evidence objective. On 2,000 clean-test questions, language model LoRA SFT changes correct-image accuracy by +1.10 percentage points (95% paired bootstrap CI:-0.85 to +3.05), while visual-benefit events decrease by 2.40 points and image sensitivity decreases by 5.60 points. Paired records reveal 155 acquired and 203 lost visual-benefit events. Broader adaptation yields lower correct-image accuracy than language-model LoRA SFT. Standard GRPO produces mixed-reward groups and parameter updates, with an uncertain clean test accuracy change. A generation audit reveals that canonical option scores can follow a different token path from generated answers. With scores taken along the greedy generation path, the evidence target improves on the training set; its gains over standard GRPO remain inconsistent on validation data at matched training doses. Sample-level analyses trace how evidence scores, decision margins, and generated answers change during post-training. This empirical and measurement audit identifies gaps between optimization activity, target acquisition, and useful held-out visual behavior.

</details>


### [77] [Diagnosing the Sources of Compositional Failure in Vision-Language Models: A Controlled Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31456)
*Mona Gandhi,Cenk Merih Olcay,Kuan-Chieh Lo,Santiago Castro,Christopher W. Myers,Srinivasan Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出COMPASS评测框架，通过对比"组合描述"与"分解描述"的VLM表现，解耦组合推理失败的多重因素，发现各技能主要受自身原始类型数量影响（自负载负效应），跨负载效应多为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在组合推理任务上表现不佳，但成因不明。常见假设是模型难以整合多组件，因而投入训练干预改善组合绑定，然而该假设从未被直接量化。现有基准仅在组合形式上评估caption，无法区分"联合推理代价"与"在负载增加下识别单个组件的代价"。

Method: 提出COMPASS（COMPositional Analysis of SkillS）受控评测框架：1) 对比组合caption与其分解对应版本，在87K图文对上直接量化组合整合代价；2) 在技能层面（目标检测、属性绑定、关系推理）使用技能定向扰动，在274K图文对上分析逐一技能的行为；3) 在标准对比编码器、显式训练的组合推理模型与非对比架构上验证模式普适性。

Result: 组合整合gap真实存在，但仅能解释观察到退化的一部分，说明还有其他因素。技能层面呈现一致模式：每个技能主要随自身原始类型数量增加而退化（self-load负效应），而跨类型负载（cross-load）效应多为正向，说明不同类型的原始元素提供了有益的grounding上下文。该模式在多种架构上均成立。

Conclusion: 组合退化反映的是多个可分离因素，不能简单归因于联合推理单一原因。研究为细粒度诊断VLM组合能力提供了受控分析工具，并修正了"整合失败是主因"的既有假设。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often struggle with compositional reasoning tasks, but the reasons for this underperformance remain unclear. A common hypothesis is that models struggle to integrate multiple components, leading to training interventions to improve compositional binding. However, this assumption has never been directly quantified. Existing benchmarks evaluate captions only in their composed form, making it impossible to separate the cost of joint reasoning from the cost of recognizing individual components under increasing load. We introduce COMPASS (COMPositional Analysis of SkillS), a controlled evaluation framework designed to isolate and measure the distinct factors underlying compositional failure. By comparing performance on composed captions with their decomposed counterparts , we directly quantify the cost of compositional integration across 87K image-caption pairs. Across multiple VLMs, this gap is real but partial, accounting for only part of the observed degradation. This motivates a finer-grained investigation into what additional factors govern model behavior. We analyze performance at the level of individual skills: object detection, attribute binding, and relation reasoning, using skill-targeted perturbations across 274K image-caption pairs. We find a consistent skill-specific pattern: each skill degrades primarily with the count of its own primitive type (self-load), while cross-load effects are predominantly positive, suggesting that primitives of different types provide useful grounding context. This pattern holds across standard contrastive encoders, explicitly trained compositional reasoning models, and non-contrastive architectures. These findings show that compositional degradation reflects multiple separable factors that cannot be reduced to joint reasoning alone.

</details>


### [78] [KneePreM: Towards 3D Knee MRI Foundation Models via Large-Scale Unlabeled Pretraining and Label-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31461)
*Xinxin Wang,Liam Hazan,Jing Li,Simona Rabinovici-Cohen,Xiaojuan Li,Mingrui Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KneePreM：基于19,011例无标注膝关节MRI预训练的3D U-Net掩码自编码器，在分类与分割下游任务中提升迁移性能与标签效率，50%-75%数据即可匹敌全量基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膝关节MRI无标注数据量庞大但利用不足，缺乏膝部专用的3D自监督预训练模型，且临床标注成本高，需要验证自监督预训练在迁移能力与标签效率上的价值。

Method: 以3D U-Net掩码自编码器在OAI的19,011例无标注MRI序列（4,791名受试者）上预训练；在fastMRI+二标签分类、APM八目标分类、SKM-TEA分割、APM分割上做全量与缩减数据微调，对照随机初始化与SuPreM；采用平衡准确率、F1、ROC AUC、PR AUC、Dice评估，统计用bootstrap置信区间、配对bootstrap检验与Wilcoxon符号秩检验；并以MCP接口实现部署流程。

Result: 全量数据下KneePreM在fastMRI+与APM上的macro ROC AUC均优于两个基线（p<.001）。fastMRI+用50%数据达ROC AUC 0.722，超过两基线的全量结果；APM用70%数据达0.740，与全量随机基线持平并优于SuPreM。SKM-TEA 70%数据Dice 0.838高于全量随机基线0.835；APM 75%数据Dice 0.746高于全量随机基线0.731，且均优于同预算对照。

Conclusion: 膝部专用3D自监督预训练可同时提升迁移性能与标签效率，在标注数据有限时尤为有效。

Abstract: Background: Large volumes of unlabeled knee MRI scans are available across repositories but remain insufficiently leveraged. We developed KneePreM, a knee-specific 3D self-supervised model, and evaluated transfer and label efficiency for classification and segmentation. Methods: A 3D U-Net masked autoencoder was pretrained on 19,011 unlabeled Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI) MRI series from 4,791 participants. Downstream fine-tuning used full and reduced training sets for fastMRI+ two-label classification (1,172 examinations), Arthroscopic Partial Meniscectomy (APM) eight-target classification (1,716 examinations), SKM-TEA segmentation (155 examinations), and APM segmentation (25 examinations). Baselines were random initialization and SuPreM. Deployment workflow was implemented with a Model Context Protocol interface. Evaluation metrics included balanced accuracy, F1 score, ROC AUC, PR AUC, and Dice score. Statistical analysis used bootstrap confidence intervals and paired bootstrap tests for classification and Wilcoxon signed-rank tests for segmentation. Results: KneePreM achieved higher full-data macro ROC AUC than both baselines for fastMRI+ and APM (all p < .001). For fastMRI+ classification, KneePreM achieved a ROC AUC of 0.722 using 50% of the training data, exceeding both full-data baselines. In APM classification, KneePreM reached a ROC AUC of 0.740 with 70% of the data, matching the full-data random baseline and outperforming SuPreM. For SKM-TEA segmentation, its 70%-data Dice of 0.838 exceeded the full-data random baseline (0.835) and both same-budget comparators. In APM segmentation, its 75%-data Dice of 0.746 exceeded the full-data random baseline (0.731) and both same-budget comparators. Conclusion: KneePreM improves transfer performance and label efficiency across knee MRI classification and segmentation tasks, particularly when labeled training data are limited.

</details>


### [79] [SatNav: A Scalable Benchmark for Long-Horizon UAV Vision-Language Navigation from Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31507)
*Jiajun Jiang,Chunliang Hua,Zichun Chen,Yanxing Wu,Zeyuan Yang,Jie Song,Xiao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatNav：基于高分辨率卫星影像构建的城市级长时程无人机视觉语言导航基准，含118K轨迹与三类任务，并提出可切换记忆模块的SwiftVLN框架，验证卫星到真机UAV的迁移可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市UAV视觉语言导航需长时记忆与地理空间grounding，但现有基准依赖昂贵重建3D资产，场景与episode规模受限于地理多样性，难以scale；因此需一种可扩展、低成本的城市级长时程基准。

Method: 以高分辨率卫星影像为数据源，用卫星俯视裁剪近似UAV下视观测；通过自动化cue-to-episode流水线从18城59场景生成118K episodes（平均轨迹379m）；定义Boundary、Landmark、Route三类任务分别考察环路进度跟踪、地标空间grounding、带计数线索的路线跟随；提出模块化框架SwiftVLN并进行记忆设计消融。

Result: 经典VLN代理与LVLM代理在该基准上均表现不佳，城市尺度导航仍具挑战；记忆设计消融给出系统性对比；卫星到UAV迁移实验表明卫星训练模型可在真实飞行UAV观测上运行。

Conclusion: SatNav以卫星影像绕过3D重建瓶颈，实现了可扩展的城市级长时程UAV VLN评测，并验证了卫星域训练到真机部署的实用相关性。

Abstract: Urban uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) vision-language navigation (VLN) requires agents to follow instructions across extended urban spaces, inherently demanding long-term memory and geospatial grounding. However, scaling existing benchmarks remains difficult because of their reliance on costly reconstructed 3D assets, limiting geographic diversity and episode scale. To address this, we introduce SatNav, a scalable, long-horizon UAV VLN benchmark built from high-resolution satellite imagery. SatNav targets city-level navigation missions and uses satellite crops as approximations of UAV nadir views for visual observations. Through an automated cue-to-episode pipeline, SatNav constructs 118K episodes from 59 scenes across 18 cities, with an average trajectory length of 379 m. To stress-test long-horizon memory and geospatial reasoning, SatNav defines three task families: Boundary, Landmark, and Route, targeting loop progress tracking, landmark-based spatial grounding, and route following with counting cues. Benchmarking classical VLN agents and recent agents based on large vision-language models (LVLMs) on SatNav shows that city-scale navigation remains challenging. We further introduce SwiftVLN, a modular framework with switchable memory components, and conduct systematic memory-design ablations. Finally, satellite-to-UAV transfer experiments show that satellite-trained navigation models can operate on real-flight UAV observations, showing the practical relevance of SatNav. Our project page: this https URL

</details>


### [80] [ClearGS: Reliability-Aware Gaussian Splatting from Handheld Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31509)
*Xuanzhi Liu,Xinyi Wu,Hang Pan,Wensi Huang,Zhenyao Wu,Ruize Han,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClearGS：面向手持视频3DGS的可信度感知视角分配与渲染引导视频内修复，无需配对清晰监督即实现SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持视频存在视角覆盖不均与帧质量参差问题，传统二值选帧会丢失轨迹覆盖或引入模糊/失真帧，且加权无法恢复已丢失细节，也缺乏干净参考图像。

Method: RVA按外观可信度、退化风险和几何效用分配分级监督权重，并弱激活有用被抑制帧以维持轨迹覆盖；RIVR以当前3DGS渲染为位姿对齐结构候选，用冻结的无参考修复专家恢复原始视频观测，再由无参考感知分数在渲染、修复观测和高频融合候选间选择；最后用全轨迹修复巩固回访已接受修复以保留早期细节。

Result: 在GS2E与GSOTM上取得总体SOTA性能，多数退化设置下CLIP-IQA、MUSIQ一致提升且LPIPS下降，且无需配对清晰监督或匹配干净参考。

Conclusion: 分级软权重视角分配结合渲染引导的免参考修复与全轨迹巩固，可在无干净参考条件下显著提升退化手持视频的3DGS重建质量。

Abstract: We present ClearGS for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) from handheld videos with uneven viewpoint coverage and mixed frame quality. Rather than selecting frames with binary decisions, ClearGS uses Reliability-aware View Allocation (RVA) to assign graded raw-supervision weights based on appearance reliability, degradation risk, and geometric utility, while weakly reactivating useful suppressed frames to maintain trajectory coverage. Since weighting cannot restore details lost to blur or distortion, ClearGS further introduces Render-Guided In-Video Restoration (RIVR). The current 3DGS render provides a pose-aligned structural candidate, a frozen no-reference restoration expert restores the corresponding raw video observation without any clean reference image, and no-reference perceptual scores select among the render, restored observation, and high-frequency fused candidate. ClearGS then applies Full-Trajectory Repair Consolidation to revisit accepted repairs and preserve details introduced early. On GS2E and GSOTM, ClearGS achieves state-of-the-art overall performance, with consistent CLIP-IQA and MUSIQ gains and LPIPS reductions in most degradation settings, without paired sharp supervision or matched clean references.

</details>


### [81] [Forensic Twins: Self-Supervised Residual Learning for AI-Generated Image Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31514)
*Javier Muñoz-Haro,Ruben Tolosana,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez,Aythami Morales,Julian Fierrez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Forensic Twins 自监督残差学习框架，仅用真实图像训练，实现零样本 AI 生成图像溯源与检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成图像检测器依赖覆盖所有生成架构的训练样本，面对新架构即失效；而常规自监督预训练的数据增强会破坏图像成像过程中的微观统计特征，与取证任务相冲突。

Method: 设计自监督残差学习（SSRL）框架，其 pretext task 抑制宏观内容信息：图像先经冻结的现成取证残差提取器，再从中抽取两个空间不相交的裁剪视图，二者不共享像素，仅保留极少语义结构用于对齐，使冗余缩减目标的主要共同信号为图像采集管线的平稳指纹。整个训练仅使用真实图像，从不接触 AI 生成图像；推理时用真实图像嵌入离线拟合 GMM 即可获得零样本检测器。

Result: 生成器来源归因准确率 56.61%，比此前零样本 SOTA 高 6.13%，延迟降低 375 倍；离线 GMM 使其成为 SOTA 零样本检测器，在 27 个未见过的 AI 生成器（含 GAN、扩散模型与商业系统）上达到 97.99% AUC。代码、权重与数据划分将公开。

Conclusion: 通过压制语义内容的残差自监督学习，可仅凭真实图像学到可迁移的图像获取指纹表征，实现跨未见过生成架构的零样本溯源与检测。

Abstract: Detectors of AI-generated images are typically trained using samples from all Generative AI architectures they must catch, and struggle as soon as a new architecture emerges. Recent approaches have explored self-supervised pre-training as an alternative solution, yet standard frameworks work against the forensic task, e.g., their augmentations overwrite the micro-statistics of image formation. This paper introduces Forensic Twins, a Self-Supervised Residual Learning (SSRL) framework whose pretext task suppresses macroscopic content availability. Each image is mapped through a frozen, off-the-shelf forensic residual extractor, from which two spatially disjoint crops are drawn. Sharing no pixel, the two views retain minimal semantic structure to align, leaving a redundancy-reduction objective with a predominant common signal: the stationary fingerprint of the image acquisition pipeline. Additionally, Forensic Twins is trained exclusively on real images; no AI-generated image is observed at any stage. Experiments show that Forensic Twins attributes AI generator sources with 56.61% accuracy, i.e., 6.13% above the previous state-of-the-art zero-shot method at 375x lower latency. We also demonstrate that fitting a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) offline using only the real image embeddings extracted from Forensic Twins turns it into a state-of-the-art zero-shot detector, reaching 97.99% AUC across 27 unseen AI generators, including GANs, diffusion models and commercial systems. Code, weights and exact splits will be made publicly available

</details>


### [82] [Structured Reasoning Agentic Framework for Interpretable Critical View of Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31524)
*Qing Xu,Yuxiang Luo,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReasonCVS：基于视觉语言模型的智能体化结构化推理框架，将腹腔镜胆囊切除术的 CVS 评估分解为细粒度解剖核验，在 Endoscapes-CVS201 上达 68.1% mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CVS 评估方法把安全性关键视野判定当作整体黑盒预测，直接将视觉特征映射到标准级标签，缺乏对解剖关系的显式推理，导致可解释性与组合泛化能力不足，而肝胆三角解剖复杂、胆管损伤风险高。

Method: 框架包含三部分：(1) Anatomical Scene Graph Abstraction (ASGA)，把解剖实体及其空间关系组织成结构化场景图表示；(2) Rationale-Aware Reasoning Agent，由经理由蒸馏微调的 LLM 驱动，作为严格中心决策者；(3) VLM 驱动的 Sub-criterion Verifier 作为专门感知工具，解析场景图并独立评估各子标准。最终通过校准软推理聚合分布观测，输出判定与可追溯的临床理由。

Result: 在 Endoscapes-CVS201 基准上取得 68.1% mAP，优于现有最优方法，同时提供可解释的标准级说明。

Conclusion: 结构化、可核验的智能体推理范式可提升手术场景理解的性能与可解释性，为可靠的手术评估提供了新路径；但验证仅限于单一医学基准，尚缺乏跨机构泛化与临床部署证据。

Abstract: Surgical scene understanding is critical for computer-assisted intervention, yet laparoscopic cholecystectomy remains challenged by the complex anatomy of the hepatocystic triangle and the risk of bile duct injury. Existing methods for Critical View of Safety (CVS) assessment typically treat it as a holistic prediction task, mapping visual features directly to criterion-level labels. This black-box paradigm lacks explicit reasoning about anatomical relationships, limiting both interpretability and compositional generalization. To address this, we propose ReasonCVS, a structured reasoning agentic framework empowered by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that decomposes CVS assessment into explicit, fine-grained anatomical verification. Specifically, we devise an Anatomical Scene Graph Abstraction (ASGA) that organizes anatomical entities and their spatial relationships into a structured representation. To operationalize this, we introduce a Rationale-Aware Reasoning Agent, powered by a Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuned via rationale distillation. Functioning as a strict central decision-maker, it invokes VLM-driven Sub-criterion Verifier as a specialized perceptual tool to parse the graph and independently evaluate individual sub-criteria. Through calibrated soft reasoning, this agent synthesizes the tool-gathered distributed observations, yielding a final verdict alongside a traceable clinical rationale. Extensive experiments on the Endoscapes-CVS201 benchmark demonstrate that ReasonCVS achieves superior performance (68.1\% mAP) over state-of-the-art while providing interpretable, criterion-level explanations for reliable surgical assessment.

</details>


### [83] [Region-Level Black-Box Defense Against Stealthy Embedding-Space Backdoors in CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31558)
*Ahmed Abdelnaby,Mohamed Elmahallawy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CLIPGuard，一种轻量级纯黑盒防御方法，通过分段嵌入扰动检测并语义修复可疑区域，抵御CLIP嵌入空间后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP的嵌入空间后门攻击只要污染极少量图文对即可植入隐蔽触发器，直接篡改表征而非分类logits，低毒化率下依然高效且难检测；现有防御依赖模型参数、梯度、logits或干净验证集等黑盒场景下难以获得的假设，且现有黑盒方法难以精确定位小目标或分布外触发器。

Method: CLIPGuard为完全黑盒防御：通过度量分段（segment-wise）嵌入扰动识别恶意区域，仅对可疑分段进行语义修复（semantic inpainting）以实现选择性净化，从而保留良性视觉内容与对齐质量；无需访问模型参数、梯度、logits或干净验证数据。

Result: 在STL-10、ImageNet及BadCLIP、BadNets、blended、patch-based、typographic等多样触发器族上，攻击成功率降至低至1.05%，干净准确率保持最高86.34%，持续优于CleanCLIP与CleanerCLIP等黑盒防御。

Conclusion: CLIPGuard以轻量、纯黑盒方式有效缓解CLIP嵌入空间后门，兼顾防御效果与干净性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Contrastive Language--Image Pretraining (CLIP) has emerged as a dominant vision backbone due to its strong transferability and zero-shot capabilities. However, recent studies reveal a critical vulnerability: embedding-space backdoor attacks. By poisoning only a tiny fraction of image--text pairs, adversaries can implant stealthy triggers that induce targeted shifts in CLIP's joint embedding space. Unlike conventional backdoors that manipulate classifier logits, these attacks corrupt representations directly, making them highly effective under extremely low poisoning ratios and difficult to detect. Existing defenses require access to model parameters, gradients, logits, or clean validation data---assumptions that rarely hold in realistic black-box deployments. Moreover, current black-box methods struggle to accurately localize small or out-of-distribution triggers. We propose CLIPGuard, a lightweight and fully black-box defense specifically designed to mitigate embedding-space backdoors in CLIP encoders. CLIPGuard identifies malicious regions by measuring segment-wise embedding perturbations and selectively purifies only suspicious segments via semantic inpainting, preserving benign visual content and alignment quality. Extensive experiments on STL-10, ImageNet, and diverse trigger families---including BadCLIP, BadNets, blended, patch-based, and typographic attacks---demonstrate that CLIPGuard reduces attack success rates to as low as 1.05% while maintaining clean accuracy up to 86.34%, consistently outperforming existing black-box defenses, including CleanCLIP and CleanerCLIP. Our code is available this https URL

</details>


### [84] [OC-GS: Gaussian Splatting for Irregular Turntable Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31572)
*Jae Joong Lee,Bedrich Benes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OC-GS 通过轨道一致的联合优化，在共享相机、旋转轴与枢轴约束下精修每张图像的拍摄角度，从而在稀疏、不规则转台拍摄条件下提升物体重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转台重建中普遍假设等角度采样，但实际存在转速不均与丢帧，导致等角度假设失效，稀疏且角度不规则的输入会严重退化重建质量。因此需要一种能在共享运动模型下修正不确定拍摄角度的物体级重建方法。

Method: 提出 OC-GS，一种 object-centric Gaussian splatting 方法。核心是 orbit-consistent refinement：在保持共享相机、旋转轴与枢轴的前提下，联合优化图像估计角度与由图像导出的几何，并采用图像角度初始化与共享运动模型两个组件（消融验证其必要性）。在统一训练器下与固定角度估计对比，并在 12/8/6 个不规则视角下与四个 pose-free Gaussian splatting 基线比较。

Result: 合成渲染物体上，12/8/6 视角平均前景 PSNR 分别为 21.26 / 19.36 / 15.83 dB，均超过四个 pose-free 基线；同一训练器下精修角度比固定估计提升 7.88 dB；真实拍摄上精修带来 0.70 dB 平均前景 PSNR 提升。

Conclusion: 在共享运动模型内精修不确定角度，可有效改善稀疏、不规则转台拍摄的重建质量；角度初始化与共享运动模型均有贡献。

Abstract: Uneven rotation and dropped frames make equal-angle assumptions unreliable for turntable reconstruction. We present OC-GS, an object-centric Gaussian splatting that refines each image's angle while maintaining a shared camera, rotation axis, and pivot. This orbit-consistent refinement jointly optimizes image-derived geometry and angles to reconstruct objects from sparse, irregular captures. On rendered objects with 12, 8, and 6 irregularly spaced views, OC-GS achieves mean foreground PSNR scores of 21.26, 19.36, and 15.83dB, respectively, exceeding all four evaluated pose-free Gaussian splatting baselines in each condition. Under a shared trainer, refining image-estimated angles improves mean foreground PSNR by 7.88dB over keeping those estimates fixed. An ablation study shows that both image-derived angle initialization and the shared motion model contribute to the improvement. On real captures, OC-GS's refinement increases mean foreground PSNR by 0.70dB. Results show that refining uncertain angles within a shared motion model improves reconstruction from sparse, irregular turntable captures.

</details>


### [85] [How Far Can INRs Go? Cross-Domain Parameter-efficient INR-Based Semantic Segmentation for Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31573)
*Ziyao Shang,Pouya Sadeghi,Letian Jiang,Alexander Wong,Sirisha Rambhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究隐式神经表示(INR)在跨域脑MRI分割中的机制、缩放行为与泛化能力，发现其优势仅在低参数量和弱增强条件下显著，并提出分层聚合多层特征的HierINRSeg架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INR分割作为轻量替代方案在参数量上优于传统架构，但其工作机理、参数缩放规律和跨域泛化能力尚缺乏系统研究，而临床部署又面临标注少、显存受限、跨站点分布漂移等实际问题。

Method: 在跨域脑MRI分割场景下，对INR分割在低参数区间进行系统分析，与U-Net等传统流程在域内/域外设置下对比；通过分析INR隐藏特征中语义信息的编码方式，发现分割相关结构分布在不同层，据此设计分层INR架构HierINRSeg聚合多层表示。

Result: INR模型并非参数量越大越好：其优势在低参数与有限增强下最明显，而U-Net更受益于大容量和标准增强；HierINRSeg在域内Dice上平均超过MetaSeg基线5.6个百分点，域外超过8.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作明确了INR分割最有效的适用条件，为模型选择与后续研究提供了具体指导；分层聚合多层表示可提升鲁棒性与泛化性。

Abstract: Biomedical image segmentation is central to medical image analysis, but practical deployment often faces limited annotations, memory constraints, and cross-site distribution shifts. Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) have recently emerged as a lightweight alternative for semantic segmentation, achieving competitive performance with substantially fewer parameters than conventional architectures. However, the mechanisms, scaling behavior, and domain generalization abilities of INR-based segmentation remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we study these questions in the context of cross-domain brain MRI segmentation. We analyze INR-based segmentation across low-parameter regimes, comparing it with conventional pipelines in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings. Surprisingly, we find that INR-based models do not simply improve with increasing parameter budget. Their advantage is most pronounced under low-parameter and limited-augmentation settings, while U-Net-based models benefit more from larger capacity and standard augmentation. We also investigate how INRs encode semantic information in their hidden features and show that complementary segmentation-relevant structure is distributed across multiple INR layers. Building on this insight, we introduce HierINRSeg, a hierarchical INR-based architecture that aggregates multi-layer representations for improved robustness and generalization. Extensive experiments show that HierINRSeg consistently outperforms MetaSeg, a strong recent INR-based segmentation baseline, with an average improvement of 5.6 percentage points in Dice for the in-domain test set and 8.2 percentage points out-of-domain. Overall, our analysis identifies the conditions under which INR-based segmentation is most effective, providing concrete guidance for model selection and future research.

</details>


### [86] [GraphWrit3R: End-to-End 3D Scene Graph Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31595)
*Luka Milivojevic,Nikola Popovic,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Sebastian Koch,Iro Armeni,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraphWrit3R：端到端 3D 场景图生成方法，直接以点云/高斯泼溅为输入输出结构化 JSON 场景图，无需多阶段管线与真值物体标注，在 3DSSG 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 场景图生成存在三大瓶颈：多阶段显式中间表示导致误差累积与系统脆弱；推理时依赖真值物体标注，偏离真实场景；依赖闭源模型难以开源部署或推理过慢。作者希望用单一端到端模型消除这些限制。

Method: GraphWrit3R 为端到端框架：点云经 Sonata 编码、高斯泼溅经 Chorus 编码，两种模态投影到共享体素栅格，通过新颖的逐体素对比对齐损失融合，再由大语言模型解码为结构化 JSON 场景图（物体、语义属性、物体间关系）。多模态输入仅为通用性考虑，单套权重可处理多种场景；借助 LLM 天然支持开放词汇查询。

Result: 在 3DSSG 基准上，物体类别、谓词与三元组召回均达 SOTA，且超越推理时依赖真值物体标注的方法。论文还给出定性结果，并分析不同输入模态配置、对比损失形式与 token 融合策略的影响。

Conclusion: 端到端、免真值标注、支持开源部署与开放词汇查询的 3D 场景图生成是可行且更优的范式；模态编码器与对比对齐损失的设计对性能有显著影响。

Abstract: 3D scene graphs provide a structured representation of complex environments by encoding objects, their semantic attributes, and the spatial and functional relationships between them. Current approaches for 3D scene graph generation suffer from several fundamental limitations. They rely on complex multi-stage pipelines with explicit intermediate representations, making systems fragile and prone to error propagation. They assume access to ground-truth object annotations during inference, which deviates from real-world scenarios. They depend on proprietary models, hindering open-source deployment, or incur prohibitively slow inference. We present GraphWrit3R, a simple end-to-end method that takes a 3D point cloud, Gaussian Splats, or a combination of both as input, and directly outputs a complete scene graph as a structured JSON script. The graph lists all objects, their semantic attributes, and the relationships between them, while avoiding all of the above mentioned limitations. The choice of multiple input modalities is purely for versatility, allowing a single set of weights to handle diverse scenarios. Point cloud inputs are encoded via Sonata and Gaussian Splat inputs via Chorus, with both modalities projected onto a shared voxel grid and fused through a novel per-voxel contrastive alignment loss before being decoded by a large language model. As a natural consequence of the LLM, GraphWrit3R also supports open-vocabulary querying. On the 3DSSG benchmark, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on object class, predicate, and triplet recall, outperforming methods that rely on ground-truth object annotations during inference. We further provide qualitative results and analyze different input modality configurations, contrastive loss formulations, and token fusion strategies.

</details>


### [87] [FuseReg: Regularizing Layer Fusion Mitigates the Reconstruction-Generation Gap in Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31620)
*Hongyang Du,Yunfei Xie,Junjie Ye,Jiawei Yang,Xiaoyan Cong,Haodong Zhang,Yongchao Huang,Haiyu Wu,Zongxia Li,Shihang Gui,Dawei Liu,Runhao Li,Jingcheng Ni,Chen Wei,Randall Balestriero,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FuseReg 用随机子集采样训练替代 RAE 中的启发式层级融合，理论证明该采样惩罚跨层不一致性，使单一解码器兼容全层/稀疏/单层潜在表示，在 ImageNet-256 上提升 PSNR 并显著降低 gFID（解码器替换降 27%，联合正则降 29%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAE 复用预训练视觉编码器特征作为重构与扩散潜在表示，但需决定哪些编码器层构成生成器与像素解码器共享的潜在空间。浅层利于像素细节，深层利于生成指标，固定启发式融合将两个本应获取不同信息的阶段耦合，存在权衡。

Method: 提出 FuseReg：不固定层融合，而是在编码器层的随机子集上训练解码器/生成器；作者从理论上分析子集采样显式惩罚对跨层分歧的敏感性。该方法可与扩散训练结合，对两个阶段联合施加正则。

Result: ImageNet-256 + DINOv3-L：单解码器无需重训即可从全层、稀疏及单层融合重构，PSNR 高于针对固定融合专门训练的解码器；仅替换解码器使 RAEv2 DiT-XL 生成器无引导 gFID 降低 27%；联合正则下 DiT-Base 无引导 gFID 降低 29%。

Conclusion: 面向层融合鲁棒性训练下游模型，可在不修改预训练编码器的前提下缩小重构—生成差距，为表示自编码器的潜在空间设计提供可泛化原则。

Abstract: Representation autoencoders (RAEs) reuse features from a pretrained visual encoder as reconstruction and diffusion latents, integrating strong visual representations into image generation. However, RAEs still need to decide which encoder layers form the shared latent space for the generator and pixel decoder. This choice involves a trade-off. Shallower layers tend to preserve fine pixel details better, while deeper layers tend to yield better generation metrics. A fixed heuristic layer fusion therefore couples two stages that benefit from different information. We introduce FuseReg, which replaces heuristic feature selection with training over random subsets of encoder layers. We theoretically analyze the underlying mechanism: subset sampling explicitly penalizes sensitivity to cross-layer disagreement. On ImageNet-256 with DINOv3-L, a single FuseReg decoder reconstructs from full, sparse, and single-layer fusions without retraining, achieving higher PSNR than decoders specialized to fixed fusions. This flexibility also benefits generation: decoder replacement alone reduces unguided gFID by 27% with an unchanged RAEv2 DiT-XL generator. The same regularization principle extends to diffusion training, with joint regularization of both stages reducing unguided gFID by 29% on DiT-Base. These results show that training downstream models for layer-fusion robustness narrows the reconstruction-generation gap without modifying the pretrained encoder.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [TinyCVIO: A Constellation-Aided Visual-Inertial Odometry System for Nanodrones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30358)
*Derin Ozturk,Kaan Akan,Irwin Wang,Christopher Batten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TinyCVIO，面向仅 520 kB SRAM 的双核 MCU 的纳米无人机视觉-惯性里程计系统，通过 LED 星座、流式视觉前端和刚体测量模型实现机载实时状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米无人机在严重受限的感知与算力条件下需要准确、实时的状态估计，现有 VIO 方案难以在毫瓦级算力和极小内存的商用 MCU 上同时满足精度与实时性。

Method: 软硬件协同设计：① 轻量 LED 星座提供已知几何（无需测绘位置或偏航角，LED 共面放置）；② 毫米级相机的流式视觉前端以 29.2 FPS 跟踪 LED 观测；③ 刚体板测量模型保留 LED 间约束；④ 流式 QR 分解将估计工作区内存界定为固定滤波器状态规模。

Result: 19 组手持硬件在环数据集上，刚体板模型相比平面点模型将平均绝对轨迹误差降低 27%；完整系统在 Crazyflie 上完成 9 次飞行（三种速度），10 m 段落平均绝对轨迹误差 3.5–3.7 cm，相对位姿误差 0.50–0.60%，平均估计延迟 15.7–16.3 ms。

Conclusion: 在商用双核 MCU 上可实现高精度、低延迟的机载视觉-惯性里程计，刚体板约束与流式 QR 是精度与内存的关键设计。

Abstract: Nanodrones require accurate, real-time state estimation under severe sensing and computational constraints. We present TinyCVIO, a visual-inertial odometry system that co-designs miniature sensing, visual processing, and estimation for a commodity dual-core microcontroller with 520 kB SRAM. Lightweight LED constellations provide known geometry without surveyed positions or yaw angles, assuming placement on a common level plane. A streaming visual frontend tracks LED observations from a millimeter-scale camera at 29.2 FPS, while a rigid-board measurement model retains inter-LED constraints and streaming QR bounds estimation workspace for a fixed filter-state size. Across 19 hand-held hardware-in-the-loop datasets, the rigid-board model reduces mean absolute trajectory error by 27% relative to planar points. The complete system runs onboard a Crazyflie across nine flights at three speeds, achieving 3.5-3.7 cm mean absolute trajectory error and 0.50-0.60% relative pose error over 10 m segments, with mean estimate latency of 15.7-16.3 ms.

</details>


### [89] [POIL: Point-based One-Shot Imitation Learning with Stable Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30404)
*Sang Min Kim,Jinwoo Seo,Hyeongjun Heo,Junho Lee,Yonghyeon Lee,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POIL 提出一种基于点的单样本模仿学习框架，利用物体功能部位上的 3D 点集作为共享表示，同时完成轨迹迁移与闭环执行，在仿真与真机上实现跨类别、跨抓取位姿的鲁棒操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单样本模仿学习虽可避免大量示教，但成功操作还需将示教轨迹迁移到新物体，并在场景变化、抓取构型和外部扰动下稳健执行，现有方法难以同时满足这两点。

Method: 以物体功能部位上的 3D 点集为核心共享表示：用多模态大语言模型定位功能部位，借助点对应关系把示教轨迹跨视角、位姿与类别迁移；执行阶段用多视角跟踪在线观测同一组点，并提出 Point-set BCSDM，将各点速度投影到由跟踪点单独计算出的单一刚体 twist 上实现闭环控制，从而把稳定动力系统从 SE(3) 位姿扩展到点集，无需已知 3D 模型或位姿估计器。

Result: 理论上在目标附近控制器退化为经典 SO(3) 势能上的梯度流，在刚体假设下继承其几乎全局收敛性；仿真与真机实验中，单次示教可跨物体类别、抓取位姿与目标几何迁移，并能在执行中从外部扰动恢复。

Conclusion: 以功能部位点集统一轨迹迁移与闭环执行，为单样本操作提供兼具泛化性和稳定性的实用框架。

Abstract: We present POIL, a point-based one-shot imitation learning framework with stable dynamical systems. While one-shot imitation avoids collecting extensive demonstrations, successful one-shot manipulation requires not only transferring a demonstrated trajectory to a novel object but also executing it robustly under changing scene conditions, grasp configurations, and external disturbances. POIL addresses both problems through a shared representation: a set of 3D points on the object's functional part, used jointly for trajectory transfer and closed-loop execution. The one-shot transfer from the demonstrated trajectory is enabled with point correspondences. POIL grounds the shared functional part with a multi-modal large language model, and transfers the trajectory across viewpoint, pose, and object category changes. During execution, multi-view tracking observes the same points online, and Point-set BCSDM drives them in closed loop by projecting per-point velocities onto a single rigid-body twist computed from the tracked points alone. This extends stable dynamical models from an SE(3) pose to a point set without requiring a known 3D model or pose estimator. We show that at the goal the controller becomes a gradient flow on the classical SO(3) potential, so its terminal phase inherits the almost-global convergence of that potential under a rigid-object assumption. Across simulation and real-robot experiments, POIL transfers a single demonstration across object category, grasp pose, and goal geometry, while recovering from external disturbances during execution. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [90] [Actively Resolving Contextual Uncertainty for Underspecified Tasks in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30428)
*Zachary Ravichandran,Jonathan Diller,Fernando Cladera,Varun Murali,George J. Pappas,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLUE框架利用LLM策略在欠指定自然语言任务下主动消解上下文不确定性，通过在线构建的语言嵌入地图将假设落地为动作，并在闭环交互中迭代验证与修正计划，在Spot机器人三处真实环境中15项任务上接近oracle表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件策略多假设目标明确、任务相关信息已由先验地图提供，但在陌生环境中面对欠指定任务时存在高上下文不确定性：机器人需同时推断任务成功的定义、哪些信息相关、以及信息是否存在。作者希望赋予机器人主动消解这类上下文不确定性的能力。

Method: 提出CLUE（Closed-Loop contextual Uncertainty rEsolution）框架：1）由LLM派生策略，对任务相关概念与潜在计划进行假设生成；2）在线构建语言嵌入地图，将假设grounding为具体动作；3）通过闭环环境交互顺序评估假设，并随新信息不断精炼计划。部署于Boston Dynamics Spot。

Result: 在三个真实室内外环境、15项需物体消歧、功能推断与遮挡推理的任务上，CLUE成功率与oracle策略差距在7个百分点以内，比无闭环反馈的LLM规划器高出4倍。消融实验表明，仅构建并查询语言增强地图不足以解决复杂上下文规划任务，其成功率约为CLUE的三分之一，且消耗的VLM token超过CLUE的10倍。

Conclusion: 闭环交互反馈是消解欠指定任务上下文不确定性的关键，静态“先建图再查询”范式在复杂上下文规划中效率与效果双低；CLUE为真实机器人上主动信息获取与计划精炼提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Foundation models provide robots with the ability to interpret natural language and reason about environmental context, yet most language-conditioned policies assume that goals are well-specified and that task-relevant information is provided upfront via a prior map. Operating in unfamiliar environments with underspecified tasks entails high contextual uncertainty: the robot must jointly infer what constitutes task success, what constitutes relevant information, and where (or whether) that information exists. We address these limitations via CLUE (Closed-Loop contextual Uncertainty rEsolution), a framework for actively resolving contextual uncertainty given underspecified tasks in natural language. CLUE uses an LLM-derived policy to hypothesize task-relevant concepts and potential plans. It then uses a language-embedded map, which is constructed online, to ground these hypotheses into actions. The policy sequentially evaluates hypotheses via closed-loop environment interaction and refines its plans as it gathers new information. We deploy CLUE on a Boston Dynamics Spot across three real indoor and outdoor environments spanning 15 tasks that require object disambiguation, functional inference, and occlusion reasoning. CLUE achieves a success rate within 7 percentage points of an oracle policy and outperforms an LLM-enabled planner without closed-loop feedback by a 4x margin. Supporting experiments demonstrate that simply building and then querying a language-enriched map is insufficient to resolve complex contextual planning tasks; these approaches achieve roughly one third the success rate of CLUE while requiring over 10x more VLM tokens. We provide additional information at this https URL .

</details>


### [91] [WALT: Learning World-Model-Aligned Latent Trajectories for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30436)
*Mingkai Jia,Jiaxin Guo,Zhijian Shu,Jiawei Xu,Mingxiao Li,Jintao Cheng,Ping Tan,Wei Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WALT，通过双分支轨迹自编码器与 REPA 式特征对齐，将冻结驾驶世界模型的语义迁移到紧凑轨迹潜空间，在 NAVSIM 上小幅提升规划指标并降低 30.5% 规划器 FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型虽能预测环境表征，但视觉预测精度高并不等于轨迹规划效果好。作者认为瓶颈在于视觉世界状态与原始几何轨迹（waypoints）之间存在表征错配，规划器难以利用世界模型中与动作相关的语义信息。

Method: 提出 World-Model Alignment for Latent Trajectories (WALT)：不改动冻结的预训练驾驶世界模型，而是用双分支轨迹自编码器把原始 waypoints 映射到紧凑轨迹潜空间，并将冻结视觉世界模型的语义知识迁移（对齐）到该轨迹空间，使动作表征捕获与未来运动和规划相关的场景级线索。同时系统研究了基于 JEPA 的潜空间学习与 REPA 式特征对齐对下游规划的影响。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 上，相比原始 waypoint 基线，NAVSIMv1 的 PDMS 由 89.4 提升到 89.8，NAVSIMv2 的 EPDMS 由 87.3 提升到 87.9，同时轨迹规划器 FLOPs 降低 30.5%。

Conclusion: 在保留世界模型表征的同时抽取与动作相关的信息，可为基于世界模型的轨迹规划提供有效接口；但绝对增益较小，方法有效性有待更强证据支撑。

Abstract: Driving world models learn rich predictive representations of the surrounding environment from visual observations, yet accurate visual prediction does not necessarily translate into effective trajectory planning. We argue that a key bottleneck lies in the mismatch between visual world states and raw geometric trajectories, which may limit the planner's ability to exploit action-relevant semantics encoded by the world model. To address this issue, we propose World-Model Alignment for Latent Trajectories (WALT), which learns a compact generative trajectory latent space by transferring information from a frozen pretrained driving world model without modifying the world model itself. Rather than directly generating raw waypoints, WALT maps them into compact representations through a dual-branch trajectory autoencoder and transfers semantic knowledge from the frozen visual world model into this trajectory space, encouraging the learned action representation to capture scene-level cues relevant to future motion and planning. Beyond our proposed formulation, we systematically study latent learning based on Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) and feature alignment following Representation Alignment (REPA) to investigate how trajectory-only representation learning affects downstream planning. We evaluate WALT on the NAVSIM benchmarks. Relative to the raw-waypoint baseline, WALT improves PDMS from 89.4 to 89.8 on NAVSIMv1 and EPDMS from 87.3 to 87.9 on NAVSIMv2 while reducing trajectory planner FLOPs by 30.5%. These results suggest that preserving world representations while extracting action-relevant information provides an effective interface for world-model-based trajectory planning.

</details>


### [92] [VkVIO: Cross-platform GPU Acceleration for Visual-Inertial Odometry with Vulkan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30459)
*Ole Hoffmann,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VkVIO 是首个跨平台、基于 Vulkan 的 GPU 加速视觉惯性里程计(VIO)方法，在保持实时因果估计与 SOTA 精度的同时，可在工作站、笔记本和廉价单板计算机上部署，并超越同硬件的 CUDA 方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIO/VI-SLAM 是机器人与 XR 状态估计的关键手段，效率决定设备的体积、散热与重量。GPU 加速可降低延迟，但现有工作局限于 CUDA，导致部署被绑定到单一厂商，限制了在嵌入式、移动端与 XR 头显上的适用性。作者希望用厂商无关的图形 API 打破这一限制。

Method: 采用为 3D 图形严苛性能需求设计的、厂商无关的 Vulkan API 进行 GPU 加速，构建跨平台的 VIO 系统 VkVIO，并输出满足实时运行要求的因果(causal)估计。

Result: 在多样设备（工作站、笔记本、极廉价单板计算机）上部署，达到 SOTA 精度，并在相同硬件上性能优于基于 CUDA 的系统。

Conclusion: Vulkan 可作为 CUDA 之外的可行 GPU 加速路径，为机器人与 XR 提供低延迟、低功耗、低成本的 VIO 方案。

Abstract: Perception in robotics and XR fundamentally relies on good state estimation. Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) and Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) are proven ways of achieving this goal in a cost-effective and accurate manner. Efficiency in these systems allows for smaller, cooler, and lighter devices. GPU acceleration is a natural approach for reducing latency, thanks to their wide availability in platforms like embedded computers, mobile phones, and XR headsets. However, previous works in the literature have limited themselves to the use of CUDA for this task, significantly reducing deployment options to a single vendor. We instead leverage the vendor-agnostic Vulkan API, originally designed for the strict performance requirements of 3D graphics applications. In this work, we present VkVIO, the first, to the best of our knowledge, cross-platform GPU-accelerated VIO method. We provide state-of-the-art accuracy with causal estimates required for real-time operation. We deploy VkVIO on a diverse range of devices spanning a workstation, a laptop, and an extremely inexpensive single-board computer, while outperforming CUDA-based systems on the same hardware. VkVIO enables possibilities for low-latency, low-power, and low-cost VIO in robotics and XR.

</details>


### [93] [Realizability Is Not Enough: Encoding, Liveness, and Auditing of Synthesized Robot Supervisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30460)
*David C. Conner,Joshua Luzier,William J. Doyle,Emma R. Faith,Aubrie B. Kooiker,Andrew J. Farney,Sebastian Fox,Evangelina Grimes,Ian G. Conner,Kyle Bloom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源流水线，从ROS 2 FlexBE监督器自动生成能力级GR(1)规约，支持假设分析、策略审计、保行为状态约简并导出可执行状态机；在四案例（含四旋翼真机）中比较枚举/one-hot编码与两种活性公式，发现枚举编码综合更快，System-Goal（无pending memory）是唯一稳定生成可执行控制器的活性处理，推荐枚举+System-Goal+逐策略审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GR(1)反应式综合虽能生成带形式化保证的高层机器人监督器，但仅证明规约可实现远不足以部署：设计者还需编码易失败能力、选择匹配重试意图的活性假设、审计综合策略并翻译为机器人软件。论文旨在弥合"形式化可实现性"与"可通过协议与结构进展检查的控制器"之间的工程鸿沟。

Method: 构建ROS 2 FlexBE的开源流水线：由能力模型生成基于能力的GR(1)规约；综合前分析环境假设（活性假设与重试意图的匹配性）；对综合所得策略进行审计（覆盖协议违反、死锁、有界失败违反、目标不可达陷阱四类结构缺陷，审计器对该四类可靠且完备）；在保能力级行为证明下做状态约简；最终导出可执行状态机。实验在四个案例（六组对比）上比较enumerated与one-hot编码、以及System-Goal与Fair-Outcome两种活性表述，其中两个案例跑在四旋翼硬件上。

Result: 在测试后端下，enumerated编码通常综合更快，但命题数更少并不可靠地预示更小的控制器或更低的符号代价；跨报告网格的两种编码中，仅System-Goal（无pending memory）被确认能产生可执行控制器，Fair-Outcome可能允许非设计者预期的可实现循环而不完成目标；状态约简保持能力级行为；审计器对四类结构缺陷可靠完备，但非通用活性验证器。

Conclusion: 对该后端与模型，推荐采用enumerated编码配合System-Goal，并对每个实际综合出的策略进行审计，因为命题数与可实现性并不能度量可部署性。该流水线将实现性验证、假设分析、策略审计与状态约简串联，缩小了形式化可实现性与可部署控制器之间的差距。

Abstract: High-level robotic supervisors coordinate capabilities whose reported outcomes determine the robot's next action. Reactive synthesis can generate such supervisors with formal guarantees, but deployment requires more than proving a Generalized Reactivity (1) (GR(1)) specification realizable. Designers must encode failure-prone capabilities, choose liveness assumptions that match retry intent, audit strategies, and translate them into robot software. We present an open-source pipeline for Robot Operating System (ROS) 2 Flexible Behavior Engine (FlexBE) supervisors that generates capability-based GR(1) specifications, analyzes assumptions before synthesis, audits strategies, reduces states with a behavior-preservation proof, and emits executable state machines. Across four case studies (six comparisons), including hardware on two quadcopter platforms, we compare enumerated and one-hot encodings and two liveness formulations. Under the tested backend, enumerated encoding usually synthesizes faster, although fewer propositions do not reliably predict smaller controllers or lower symbolic cost. System-Goal without pending memory is the only liveness treatment confirmed to yield executable controllers under both encodings across the reported grid; Fair-Outcome can permit realizable cycles without designer-intended completion. For this backend and model, we recommend enumerated encoding with System-Goal and auditing every realized strategy, since proposition count and realizability do not measure deployability. The auditor is sound and complete for four structural defect classes (protocol violations, deadlocks, bounded-failure violations, goal-unreachable traps) but is not a general liveness verifier, and the reduction preserves capability-level behavior. Together, these stages narrow the gap between formal realizability and controllers that pass protocol and structural-progress checks.

</details>


### [94] [DGT-Map: Directional Global Traversability Mapping Utilizing Multi-Task Learning for Heterogeneous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30461)
*Jaskrit Singh,Kashif K. Noori,Jing Xiao,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DGT-MAP：自监督学习全局、方向相关、车辆条件化的可通行性代价地图，用共享多任务主干+车辆专属预测头，推理时输出按朝向索引的costmap，供方向感知规划器使用；仿真中集成Hybrid A*，在上下坡非对称、脊状障碍等挑战地形上导航成功率最高或并列最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局地图对每个位置只赋予单一各向同性代价，忽略了可通行性的方向依赖性与车辆特异性；而已有的学习型估计器通常按车辆独立训练，保留了车辆特性却无法共享共用地形表征，数据效率与泛化受限。

Method: 自监督框架，输入RGB-D观测与运动信号（locomotion signals）；共享多任务主干学习跨训练车辆的共用地形特征，车辆专属预测头保留平台相关响应；推理阶段生成朝向索引（heading-indexed）的方向化代价地图，交由方向感知规划器使用。

Result: 在仿真中集成到Hybrid A*导航栈，在挑战地形（下坡可通行而上坡不可、脊状障碍对部分车辆可通行而对其他车辆不可）上评测下游任务成功率；相比几何、二值、以及方向无关的学习基线，DGT-MAP取得最高或并列最高的导航成功率。

Conclusion: 将可通行性建模为方向相关且车辆条件化的全局代价地图，并通过共享主干实现跨车辆表征共享，是同时解决方向各向同性与车辆割裂训练两条局限的可行路径，可提升方向感知导航的下游任务成功率。

Abstract: Off-road traversability is direction-dependent and vehicle specific, yet most global maps assign a single isotropic cost to each location. Existing learned estimators are also commonly trained independently for each vehicle; this preserves vehicle-specific behavior but prevents vehicles from sharing common terrain representations. DGT-MAP addresses both limitations through a self-supervised framework that learns global, directional, and vehicle-conditioned traversability costmaps from RGB-D observations and locomotion signals. A shared multi-task backbone learns common terrain features across training vehicles while vehicle-specific prediction heads preserve platform-dependent responses. At inference, DGT-MAP produces a heading-indexed costmap that can be used by a direction-aware planner. We evaluate DGT-MAP in simulation by integrating it into a Hybrid A* navigation stack and measuring downstream task success on challenging terrains, including slopes that are traversable downhill but not uphill and a ridge obstacle that is traversable by some vehicles, but not by others. Across evaluated tasks, DGT-MAP achieves the highest or tied-highest navigation success rate when compared against geometric, binary, and learned direction-agnostic baselines.

</details>


### [95] [Policy-Calibrated DAgger: Offline Calibrated Noise Injection for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30462)
*Jenny Wang,George Kantor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Policy-Calibrated DAgger，利用扩散策略自身预测动作分布的方差离线估计噪声水平，借助部分去噪引导与扩散模型性质校正误差度量，在多模态动作空间下实现无需网格搜索的DAgger数据采集，性能优于无噪声DAgger并匹敌事后最优噪声水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略闭环执行会累积误差、偏离训练分布（协变量偏移）。已有DAgger类方法需在策略失败处补数据：一是在不安全状态下采集，二是需人工选定合适的噪声分布去收集专家演示。作者希望免除噪声水平的启发式选取，改为从生成式策略自身性质中离线推断所需噪声。

Method: 核心是Policy-Calibrated DAgger：1）沿专家轨迹观测处测量扩散策略预测动作的扩散程度（spread），并测量其相对记录轨迹的闭环误差；2）在多模态动作空间下误差度量失效，故在闭环控制中通过部分去噪（partial denoising）将策略引导至轨迹，再用扩散模型性质将被引导条件下测得的误差"反归一化"回未引导情形；3）据此离线标定噪声水平，用于生成新专家演示。

Result: 在狭窄杂乱环境中机器人到达发动机拉杆的任务上，于3D照片级真实感模拟器与2D平面reacher环境中评测：本方法优于未加噪的数据集聚合（DAgger）基线，并与事后（hindsight）已知的最优噪声水平性能相当，且无需对噪声水平做扫描调参。

Conclusion: 利用生成式策略预测分布的自洽性可离线校准DAgger噪声，避免不安全探索和噪声超参搜索，为多模态策略的闭环数据采集提供了有原则的替代方案；目前验证限于仿真环境。

Abstract: Policies trained with imitation learning can accumulate errors over time, causing the robot to drift outside the training distribution. Existing methods mitigate this covariate shift by collecting additional data where the policy fails or is likely to fail. The first places the robot in unsafe conditions and the second requires choosing an appropriate noise distribution to collect new expert demonstrations under that noise. We propose Policy-Calibrated DAgger, a method that makes use of the properties of recent generative policies to estimate the policy's noise offline by using its own predicted action distribution. We measure a diffusion policy's spread of predicted actions at observations along the expert trajectory and measure its closed-loop error relative to a recorded trajectory. To address issues with measuring error in a multimodal action space, we guide the policy towards the trajectory during closed-loop control through partial denoising, and use properties of a diffusion model to unnormalize the measured error as if we did not guide it. We experiment in a scenario where a robot is tasked to reach an engine lever in a cluttered and narrow environment and show results in a 3D photorealistic simulator and a 2D planar reacher environment. We show that our method surpasses policies trained with dataset aggregation without noising and matches the performance of the best noise level in hindsight, without requiring a sweep over noise levels.

</details>


### [96] [Learning-Based Pressure Predictive Control of a Vertebraic Soft Robotic Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30479)
*Wenjian Yang,Nan Huang,Yukang Nie,Fang Chen,Wanchao Chi,Jiansheng Dai,Sicong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于LSTM的压力预测控制（PPC）框架，含IK、FK与压力补偿三个模型，实现脊椎式软体机器尾的非静态与准静态运动控制，并与四足机器人协调运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人连续体结构与非线性材料行为导致运动学建模困难，尤其在非静态运动中；同时软尾需实时响应以匹配四足躯干运动速率。

Method: PPC框架由逆运动学（IK）、正运动学（FK）与压力补偿（P-comp）模型组成；以LSTM学习从目标位姿到驱动压力的非线性映射，并用预测数据集训练以实现下一时刻动作预测，用于软尾与四足机器人的协调控制。

Result: 相比仅用IK的模型，PPC仿真轨迹平均RMSE降低69.8%；在软尾-四足协调运动中，用预测数据集训练PPC可实现下一时刻动作预测，计算时间减少60.9%，提升软尾实时响应能力。

Conclusion: PPC为软尾的非静态与准静态运动控制提供了简单有效的建模方法，使软尾四足机器人具备与环境交互的功能。

Abstract: Soft robots have attracted much attention for their safe human-robot interaction and flexibility, but the typical continuum structure and nonlinear material behavior make the kinematics modelling complex, especially in non-static motions. In this work, we proposed an LSTM-based pressure predictive control (PPC) for the motion control of a vertebraic soft robotic tail and the coordination with a quadruped robot. The PPC consists of an inverse kinematics (IK) model, a forward kinematics (FK) model and a pressure compensation (P-comp) model, and achieves non-static and quasi-static motion control of the tail. Compared with the IK-only model, the average RMSE of the PPC's simulation trajectories reduces by 69.8%, when executing target trajectories. In the coordinated motions of the soft tail quadruped, using a prediction data set to train the PPC enables next-moment action prediction and reduces computation time by 60.9%, which enhances the real-time response of the tail to match the quadruped torso's moving rate. The PPC provides a simple and effective method to model the soft tail for both non-static and quasi-static motion control, and grants the soft tail quadruped with the functionality of interacting with the environment.

</details>


### [97] [Memory-Aware Multi-Sensor Perception for Efficient and Safe Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30495)
*Jingshuo Li,Yifan Xue,Yifei Li,Shubhodeep Shiv Aditya,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出记忆感知多传感器导航框架，融合LiDAR与RGB感知、在线距离场表征学习与阶段自适应MCBF-QP控制器，实现利用历史观测几何的避障与高效导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于感知的导航方法依赖先验地图或短时程观测，无法充分利用先前观测到的环境结构，导致在未知环境中的避障与目标到达效率受限。

Method: 框架包含三部分：LiDAR+RGB多传感器感知、在线距离场（distance-field）表征学习以持久表示静态基础设施、以及阶段自适应调制控制障碍函数二次规划（MCBF-QP）。控制器利用已观测几何进行绕障，并根据局部条件自适应调整安全约束与引导项，同时跟踪动态障碍物。

Result: 在复杂室内外环境中实验，导航效率与目标到达性能提升，并在狭窄通道及动态障碍物场景下保持避障能力。

Conclusion: 记忆感知表征与自适应CBF控制的结合可提升未知环境下自主导航的持久感知与避障性能。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in previously unseen environments requires effective perception, persistent environmental representation, and collision avoidance while maintaining progress toward a goal. Existing perception-based methods often rely on prior maps or short-horizon observations, limiting their ability to exploit previously observed structure. We propose a memory-aware multi-sensor navigation framework that integrates LiDAR and RGB perception, online distance-field representation learning, and a stage-adaptive Modulated Control Barrier Function Quadratic Program (MCBF-QP). The framework persistently represents static infrastructure while tracking dynamic obstacles, enabling the MCBF-QP controller to exploit previously observed geometry for obstacle circumvention and adapt its safety constraints and guidance to local conditions. Experiments in complex indoor and outdoor environments demonstrate improved navigation efficiency and goal-reaching performance while maintaining collision avoidance in narrow passages and around dynamic obstacles.

</details>


### [98] [Tactile Sensing Array for Multi-Phalanx Sensing in Humanoid Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30506)
*Neel Adwani,Muhaiminul Islam Akash,Rituja Bhattacharya,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于人形手指的低成本全指触觉阵列（Velostat+导电胶带），指尖含7个接触点（2x3矩阵环绕前/左/右面+尖端单独点），近端与中端指节各含一条正面接触线；通过重复加载与按压-释放实验测量迟滞与恢复时间，并验证带间隙/凸起的柔性3D打印接触结构可将恢复时间较平铺基线缩短74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形手抓取与操作需要整根手指的触觉反馈，而非仅指尖；然而视觉与本体感觉无法可靠提供该信息，尤其当手自身手指遮挡相机对抓取区域的视野时。因此需要低成本、可覆盖多指节的触觉传感方案，并解决压阻式传感器固有的迟滞与恢复慢的问题。

Method: 在Velostat与导电胶带基础上设计制作人形手指触觉阵列：指尖7个接触点（2x3矩阵环绕前、左、右三面，加尖端1点），近端与中端指节各1条正面接触线。通过重复加载与按压-释放测试测量迟滞与释放后恢复时间；设计并测试带间隙与凸起的柔性3D打印接触结构（受前人同类设计启发），与平铺接触基线对比；测试不同接触几何（平面、棱边、角）下的激活模式差异，并在握持动作中验证三指节均能记录接触。

Result: 带间隙与凸起的柔顺3D打印接触结构相较平铺接触基线将恢复时间缩短74%；传感器能对平面、棱边、角等不同接触几何在指尖产生可区分的激活模式；在握持过程中可记录三个指节的接触。

Conclusion: 该低成本Velostat触觉阵列可覆盖人形手指多指节并提供接触几何分辨能力，机械结构改进能显著改善恢复时间；但制作方式仍受残余迟滞限制，作者指出基于软件的补偿是未来更直接解决迟滞问题的有前景方向。

Abstract: Humanoid hands require tactile feedback across the whole finger, not just the fingertip, to grasp and manipulate objects properly. Vision and proprioception alone cannot reliably provide this information, particularly when the hand's own fingers occlude the camera's view of the grasp. We present a low-cost tactile array for a humanoid finger, made from Velostat and conductive tape. The fingertip carries seven contact points including a 2x3 matrix wrapped across its front, left, and right faces, and a separate contact point at the tip. The proximal and middle phalanges each carry a single front-facing contact line. We measure the sensor's hysteresis and recovery time after release, through repeated loading and press-release tests. We also test a compliant, 3D-printed contact structure with a gap and a bump, inspired by similar designs in prior work, and show it cuts recovery time by 74% compared to a flush-contact baseline. We then show the sensor can produce distinct activation patterns for different contact geometries (flat, edge, corner) at the fingertip, and that it registers contact across all three phalanges during a grip. Finally, we discuss the limits of our fabrication changes and point to software-based compensation as a promising way to more directly fix the remaining hysteresis in the future.

</details>


### [99] [Aerial Manipulation in the Wild with Onboard Perception, Policy Learning, and Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30521)
*Yuanzhu Zhan,Yufei Jiang,Zemu Zhang,Junyi Geng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出真实户外空中操作框架，融合扩散策略模仿学习、机载LiDAR-惯性状态估计与全身模型预测控制，实现无外部动捕条件下的户外操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外空中操作同时面临可靠状态估计、稳定飞行与外部扰动下精确操作的多重要求；现有工作多依赖室内动捕基础设施，难以在户外真实环境部署。

Method: 系统由三部分组成：(1) 从操作示教训练Diffusion Policy，基于机载观测生成期望末端执行器运动；(2) 全身MPC联合协调飞行平台与机械臂，跟踪学习到的末端轨迹；(3) 机载LiDAR-惯性里程计替代外部动捕进行状态估计。

Result: 在实体空中操作平台上验证完整框架，成功执行户外操作任务；实验表明示教驱动的操作策略可与机载状态估计及基于模型的全身控制有效集成。

Conclusion: 证明模仿学习策略、机载估计与全身MPC的结合可将空中操作从受控室内扩展到户外真实场景。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation in outdoor environments remains challenging due to the simultaneous requirements of reliable state estimation, stable aerial motion, and precise manipulation under external disturbances. In this work, we present a real-world outdoor aerial manipulation framework that integrates imitation learning, onboard LiDAR-inertial state estimation, and whole-body model predictive control. A Diffusion Policy is trained from manipulation demonstrations to generate desired end-effector motions from onboard observations. These learned commands are executed by a whole-body MPC that jointly coordinates the aerial platform and manipulator to realize the desired end-effector trajectory. To eliminate reliance on external motion-capture infrastructure, the platform employs onboard LiDAR-inertial odometry for state estimation during outdoor operation. We validate the complete framework on a physical aerial manipulator and demonstrate successful execution of outdoor manipulation tasks. The experimental results show that demonstration-driven manipulation policies can be effectively integrated with onboard state estimation and model-based whole-body control to enable aerial manipulation beyond controlled indoor environments.

</details>


### [100] [Containing Behavioral Cascades from Manipulated Claims in LLM-Powered Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30523)
*Waleed Bin Khalid,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出主动验证框架，将LLM多机器人系统遭语义操纵后的验证问题建模为团队级规划问题，通过"验证-适配-保持"结构化计划在舰队范围内遏制级联效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多机器人系统对语义操纵脆弱：一旦接受虚假世界状态声明，可能触发全舰队的行为级联，导致不必要的重规划、路径成本上升、拥堵甚至任务不可行。既有做法多把验证视为二元信任决策，缺乏对下游物理后果的遏制机制。

Method: 提出主动验证（active verification）框架，前提假设为操纵已成功，目标是遏制其传播。由专门的验证模块生成结构化Verify-Adapt-Hold计划：选定机器人核查关键区域，有限子集在必要时临时适配，其余机器人保留原可信计划。在多机器人运输环境注入虚假障碍声明，跨不同影响程度与团队规模进行评估。

Result: 评估指标包括级联遏制程度、Sum-of-Costs、makespan与覆盖率。结果显示，将受损后验证视为团队级规划问题而非二元信任决策，可有效限制语义操纵带来的级联物理后果。

Conclusion: 语义操纵防御应从二元信任判断转向团队级规划：通过分配验证、适配与保持角色，可在低成本下遏制级联失效，为LLM多机器人系统的鲁棒性提供可扩展的规划式范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered multi-robot systems are vulnerable to semantic manipulation: an accepted false world-state claim can trigger a fleet-wide behavioral cascade, causing unnecessary replanning, increased path costs, congestion, or apparent mission infeasibility. Conditioning on a successful manipulation, we propose an active verification framework that contains its downstream effects before they propagate across the fleet. A dedicated verification module generates a structured Verify-Adapt-Hold plan: selected robots inspect consequential regions, a limited subset provisionally adapts when necessary, and the remaining robots retain their trusted plans. We evaluate the framework in a multi-robot transportation environment using injected false obstacle claims across different impacts and team sizes. Evaluation measures cascade containment, Sum-of-Costs, makespan, and coverage ratio. Results show that treating post-compromise verification as a team-level planning problem, rather than a binary trust decision, effectively limits the cascading physical consequences of semantic manipulation.

</details>


### [101] [A Long-Legged, Direct-Drive Monopedal Robot Achieves Exceptional Jump Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30530)
*Gihyeok Na,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无弹性储能、由直驱传动驱动的单腿跳跃机器人，凭借新型织物缠绕可变机械利益传动，实现7.6 m高、190 ms起跳的跳跃，并利用顶部螺旋桨模块完成姿态控制，通过室外实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电驱动跳跃机器人多依赖弹性储能（弹簧）来放大输出，限制了可控性与结构简化；作者希望验证在不使用弹性储能的前提下，单纯依靠直驱电机与可变机械利益传动，能否实现高速、高跳的跳跃，并解决细长腿的屈曲与空中姿态控制问题。

Method: 采用单条长腿+直驱传动结构；核心是新型fabric-wrap传动，提供可变机械利益，使小电机在全行程中保持接近峰值功率输出，同时对细长腿提供防屈曲支撑；腿顶端设置平衡模块，用小型螺旋桨在地面与空中控制腿的姿态，利用长腿提供的大力矩臂放大控制力矩。

Result: 机器人质量281 g，跳跃高度7.6 m，起跳支撑相时间190 ms，在电驱动、无弹簧系统中达到已报道最高跳跃高度；室外验证了垂直跳跃、地面与飞行中的姿态控制以及带倾角跳跃。

Conclusion: 证明无需弹性储能即可通过可变机械利益直驱传动实现高动态跳跃，长腿+螺旋桨组合为空中姿态控制提供了有效的大力矩臂方案，具备室外实用性。

Abstract: We demonstrate a jumping robot that reaches high (7.6 m) and fast (190 ms stance time) jumps from a single long leg driven by a direct-drive transmission, without elastic energy storage. At 281 g, it achieves the highest jump yet reported for an electrically actuated, spring-free system. The leg uses a new fabric-wrap transmission that provides a variable mechanical advantage, keeping a small electric motor near its peak power output through the stroke while bracing the long, lightweight leg against buckling. A balancing module at the top of the leg uses small propellers to control the leg's orientation on the ground and in the air, where the long leg provides a large moment arm for the control torques. The robot is validated outdoors with vertical jumps, attitude control on the ground and in flight, and tilted jumps.

</details>


### [102] [ST-pRRTC: Parallel Space-Time RRT-C with Adaptive Goal-Time Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30533)
*Duo Zhang,Jintong Li,Junshan Huang,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GPU并行时空RRT-Connect规划器ST-pRRTC，采用共享前向树+自适应后向目标时间树森林，支持已知障碍轨迹、到达时间未知的动态避障问题，并在真机UR5e上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态障碍环境下的运动规划中，障碍轨迹已知但到达时间未指定；在多个到达时间上搜索可扩大时间覆盖，但会把有限规划预算分摊到更多后向树上，导致单树搜索效率下降。

Method: ST-pRRTC构建一棵共享前向树与一个自适应后向目标时间树森林：其"区间根"形式化方法对到达时间进行连续采样，保证在有界时间域内、给定假设下的概率完备性与渐进到达时间最优性；实用的根回收策略不保证上述性质，它以固定数量的后向树自适应替换后加入的根，同时保留已有搜索进展；整体算法在GPU上并行实现。

Result: 在三个动态基准上，区间根与根回收两个变体的平均首次解时间更短、平均最终到达时间早于ST-RRT*和SI-RRT；进一步实验表明在宽到达时间范围下根回收更有优势；UR5e在移动Crazyflie四旋翼间规划运动的真机演示成功。

Conclusion: 共享前向树加自适应后向树森林的结构可缓解多到达时间搜索的预算分散问题，连续时间根采样提供理论保证，根回收策略在宽时间范围下更具实用性，并在真机动态场景中得到验证。

Abstract: We propose ST-pRRTC, a GPU-parallel space- time RRT-Connect motion planner for problems with known obstacle trajectories and unspecified arrival time. Searching over many arrival times broadens temporal coverage but divides a finite planning budget among more backward trees. To address the challenge, ST-pRRTC builds a shared forward tree and an adaptive forest of backward goal-time trees. Its interval root formulation samples goal arrival times continuously and guarantees probabilistic completeness and asymptotic arrival- time optimality under the stated assumptions in a bounded time domain. The practical root recycling policy has no such guar- antees. It adapts a fixed number of backward trees, replacing later roots while retaining useful search progress. Experiments on three dynamic benchmarks show that both variants achieve lower mean first-solution times and earlier mean final arrivals than ST-RRT* and SI-RRT on problems solved by all compared methods. Further experiments demonstrate the benefit of recy- cling over broad arrival-time ranges. Real-robot demonstrations show root-recycling ST-pRRTC planning motions for a UR5e among moving Crazyflie quadrotors.

</details>


### [103] [GraspTwin: Zero-Shot Task-Oriented Grasp Optimization via a Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30543)
*Daniel J. Evans,Yinlong Dai,Simon Stepputtis,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 real-to-sim-to-real 框架：从单张 RGB-D 构建数字孪生，用基础模型生成任务导向的抓取候选（语义先验），再通过贝叶斯优化+Thompson 采样与域随机化物理回放并行精化，实现零样本真实机器人任务导向抓取。


<details>
