We evaluate the resulting jump forces in 2D force space and demonstrate that the optimized morphology is capable of continuous climbing at a speed of 0.375 m/s (1.97 body lengths/s), and can also achieve ground locomotion and transition to a vertical surface. Our work proposes design insights for the jump-climbing maneuver and serves as an important step toward creating climbing robots with agility on par with that of animals.

</details>


### [138] [Precision at Speed: Sample-Efficient Online Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Hydraulic Excavator Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31025)
*Claudio Canales,Fang Nan,Marco Hutter,Javier Ruiz-del-Solar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在线基于模型的强化学习框架，从零学习概率动力学集成模型用于采样MPC，以精度门控轮廓目标优先保证路径精度，在11.5吨液压挖掘机上20分钟实机学习即达到此前100-150分钟训练控制器的跟踪精度，40分钟后在高速下保持亚厘米级路径误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂驱动动力学机器人的高速高精度控制仍很困难，直接在硬件上学习又受真实交互成本限制；现有学习控制器需要大量实机数据或演示/仿真预训练。

Method: 在线基于模型的强化学习框架：从零学习概率动力学集成（ensemble）模型，供采样式模型预测控制（MPC）使用；设计精度门控的轮廓跟踪（contouring）目标，将进度奖励与路径精度挂钩，从而在精度与速度权衡中优先保精度；无演示、无仿真预训练，直接在实机在线学习。

Result: 在数据驱动的挖掘机模拟器中样本效率优于所评估的基于模型RL基线；在11.5吨Menzi Muck M445液压挖掘机上实机验证，20分钟交互后跟踪精度与训练100-150分钟的既有学习控制器相当，40分钟后在高速作业下维持亚厘米级平均路径误差。

Conclusion: 在线学习动力学集成+精度门控MPC可实现无需先验知识的实机高精度高速控制，显著降低机器人学习对交互数据的依赖，为重型液压装备自主作业提供可行路径。

Abstract: Precise, high-speed control remains challenging for robots with complex actuation dynamics. Learning directly on hardware is further constrained by the cost of real-world interaction. We present an online model-based reinforcement learning framework that learns a probabilistic dynamics ensemble model from scratch for sampling-based model predictive control. A precision-gated contouring objective conditions the progress reward on path accuracy, prioritizing precision over speed. In a data-driven excavator simulator, the framework achieves higher sample efficiency than the evaluated model-based reinforcement learning baselines. We validate the framework by learning directly on an 11.5-ton Menzi Muck M445 hydraulic excavator, without demonstrations or simulation pretraining. After 20 minutes of interaction, the controller reaches tracking accuracy comparable to prior learned controllers trained on 100-150 minutes of data. After 40 minutes, it sustains sub-centimeter mean path error at high operating speeds.

</details>


### [139] [Compact Force Sensor for Dual-UAV Cable-Suspended Payload Transport with Tension-Aware Outer-Loop Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31035)
*Andrea Delbene,Giorgio Cannata,Giorgio Carlini,Filippo Sante,Marco Baglietto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双无人机协同载荷运输框架，核心是自研紧凑型力传感器测量缆绳锚点交互力，配合级联控制（内环姿态稳定+外环力补偿），并通过仿真与室内实验在位置不确定、载荷脱落和受限空间场景下验证，优于文献中的分布式参考方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机协同吊运存在稳定性、协调性与鲁棒性问题，尤其在外界扰动和未建模动力学下难以保持。现有分布式控制缺少对缆绳交互力的直接、低成本测量手段，因此需要一种可集成于机体的力传感方案来改善力前馈/反馈补偿。

Method: 设计并标定一款紧凑型定制力传感器，用于测量无人机缆绳锚点处的交互力，给出传感器结构与数学模型，并通过静态与动态实验评估线性度、迟滞、重复性和交叉载荷。控制采用级联架构：内环快速回路负责机体稳定，外环回路基于测得的力进行扰动补偿。通过仿真与室内实验（含位置不确定性）验证，并开展载荷脱落与受限空间测试，与文献中的分布式方法对比。

Result: 仿真与室内实验表明所提传感+控制架构在稳定性、协调性和抗扰能力上均优于文献分布式参考方案；传感器在静态/动态测试中表现出可接受的线性度、低迟滞、良好重复性和可接受的交叉载荷特性。

Conclusion: 将定制力传感与级联力补偿控制结合的框架能有效提升双无人机协同吊运的鲁棒性与协调性，为多机协同载荷运输提供可行的硬件-控制协同设计思路。

Abstract: Cooperative payload transportation using multiple \textit{Unmanned Aerial Vehicles} (UAVs) poses challenges in stability, coordination, and robustness, especially under external disturbances and unmodeled dynamics. This work proposes a dual-UAV payload transportation framework supported by a compact, custom-designed force sensor measuring the interaction force at the UAV cable anchor point. The sensor design and mathematical model are presented, and its performance is characterized through static and dynamic tests evaluating linearity, hysteresis, repeatability, and crossload. The control architecture follows a cascade structure: fast inner loops handle vehicle stabilization, while outer loops are designed to compensate for the measured forces. The approach is validated through simulations and indoor experiments under position uncertainty. Payload-drop and constrained-space tests assess the proposed sensing and control architecture against literature-based distributed references, showing improved stabilization, coordination, and disturbance rejection. A video of the experiments is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [140] [Kintsugi-VLA: Turning Failed Robot Rollouts into Recovery Data through Interventional Recoverability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31048)
*Ivan Snegirev,Elizaveta Semenyakina,Dmitrii Maliukov,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kintsugi-VLA 将在仿真中被丢弃的失败 rollout 转化为有针对性的恢复训练数据：通过在仿真器中恢复并分支状态、以自适应蒙特卡洛加 Wilson 区间估计"干预可恢复性"，定位低可恢复性前沿并据此选取恢复起始状态，从而提升 VLA 的恢复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真 VLA 训练流水线通常只保留成功示范，丢弃失败 rollout；然而失败轨迹恰恰包含了必须学会恢复的偏离标称状态。作者希望把这些被浪费的经验结构化为恢复训练数据。

Method: 1) 借助仿真器的精确状态恢复与分支能力构造干预实验；2) 对固定特权专家定义"干预可恢复性"= 状态恢复后完成原任务的概率；3) 用自适应蒙特卡洛续跑加逐点 Wilson 区间进行估计；4) 刻画其沿失败轨迹的非单调演化，识别终止低可恢复性前沿；5) 以此为准则挑选有信息量的恢复起始状态，生成定向恢复数据并训练 SmolVLA。

Result: 在仿真 Franka 操作任务中，定向恢复数据在使用难度匹配和帧预算匹配时分别取得 34.6% 与 38.4% 的 SmolVLA 恢复成功率，在同一恢复窗口内比均匀采样高 5.8 和 6.7 个百分点；在扰动端到端执行以及改变的杂乱度与物理条件下排序一致；干净任务成功率从 76.8% 降至 74.7%。

Conclusion: 通过直接干预式测量，失败仿真 rollout 可从被丢弃经验转化为结构化恢复训练数据，为 VLA 数据增强提供了一条可测量、可复用的路径；代价是干净任务成功率的小幅下降，且结论目前限于仿真任务。

Abstract: Simulation enables scalable training of Vision-Language-Action policies by using privileged experts to generate visual demonstrations without requiring every trajectory to be collected through manual teleoperation. However, such pipelines typically retain successful demonstrations while failed rollouts are discarded, even though they expose precisely the off-nominal states from which recovery must be learned. We introduce Kintsugi-VLA, a framework for converting failed rollouts into targeted synthetic recovery data by exploiting exact state restoration and branching in simulation. For a fixed privileged expert, we define interventional recoverability as the probability of completing the original task after the simulator is restored to a given state, estimate it using adaptive Monte Carlo continuations with pointwise Wilson intervals, and characterize its non-monotonic evolution along failed trajectories. These estimates identify an observed terminal low-recoverability frontier-the point after which measured recoverability remains below a threshold-which is then used to select informative recovery starting states. In a simulated Franka manipulation task, targeted recovery data yield aggregate SmolVLA recovery success of 34.6\% and 38.4\% under difficulty- and frame-budget matching, respectively, 5.8 and 6.7 percentage points above uniform sampling within the same recovery window. The same ordering is observed under disturbed end-to-end execution and shifted clutter and physics conditions, while clean-task success decreases from 76.8\% to 74.7\%. Kintsugi-VLA demonstrates how failed simulator rollouts can be transformed from discarded experience into structured recovery-training data through direct interventional measurement.

</details>


### [141] [Accuracy Evaluation of INS/ZUPT Filtering Methods Based on Different Geometric Error Definitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31057)
*Wei Ouyang,Jiale Han,Yarong Luo,Maoran Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从不变滤波、双帧群滤波与等变滤波三种几何滤波框架出发，为参考系下表达的零速度测量推导左误差过程模型与量测模型，并针对左切群等变误差引入新的群运算；分析与INS/ZUPT实验表明TFG-IEKF与TG-EqF相比IEKF无显著一致性优势，且小初始姿态误差下传统间接EKF与三种几何滤波定位精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统间接卡尔曼滤波采用加性误差定义，缺少状态相关性，难以保证滤波一致性；几何滤波通过特定群运算定义误差状态以引入该相关性，但不同几何误差框架（不变、双帧群、等变）在零速度量测下的推导与一致性优劣尚不明确，尤其左切群等变误差缺少合适的群运算定义。

Method: 针对参考系下表达的零速度量测，分别基于不变滤波（IEKF）、双帧群滤波（TFG-IEKF）和等变滤波（TG-EqF）推导左误差的过程模型与量测模型；为左切群等变误差定义一种新的群运算；并开展INS/ZUPT组合系统实验对比四类滤波器的一致性指标与定位精度。

Result: 分析显示TFG-IEKF与TG-EqF相对IEKF并无显著一致性优势；INS/ZUPT实验中，在小初始姿态误差条件下，传统间接EKF的闭环位置误差低于行进距离的0.1%，三种几何滤波达到与之相当的定位精度。

Conclusion: 几何滤波带来的状态相关性与一致性收益在零速度量测场景中被高估：左误差框架下TFG-IEKF、TG-EqF并不优于IEKF，且整体上未超越经典间接EKF的定位表现，说明一致性优势依赖于具体误差定义与量测类型，需谨慎选择滤波模型。

Abstract: Geometric filters have recently been introduced to improve the accuracy and consistency of inertial-based integrated navigation systems. Error states were defined through specific group operations, introducing state correlations in error definition, which were lacked in the additive error used by a conventional indirect Kalman filter. The desirable consistent filtering models can be obtained based on specific geometric errors. For zero-velocity measurements expressed in the reference frame, this paper derives left-error process and measurement models from invariant filtering, two-frame-group filtering, and equivariant filtering. Importantly, a new group operation is introduced for the left tangent-group equivariant error. The analysis shows that the two-frame-group invariant extended Kalman filter (TFG-IEKF) and the tangent-group equivariant filter (TG-EqF) do not offer a significant consistency advantage over the invariant extended Kalman filter (IEKF). Experiments with an INS/ZUPT measurement system show that, under small initial attitude errors, the conventional indirect extended Kalman filter (EKF) achieves loop-closure position errors below $0.1\%$ of the traveled distance, while the three geometric filters achieve comparable positioning accuracy.

</details>


### [142] [AuthGuard-R: Safety-Compliant Mission Hijacking and Dual-Gate Defense for LLM-Controlled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31110)
*Saidattu Chepuri,Vikas Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"安全合规任务劫持"威胁（动作物理安全但违背用户授权任务），构建MissionPAIR自适应攻击框架与AuthGuard-R确定性授权层（双门架构），形式化证明多项安全性质，实验显示授权层完全拦截了所有未授权动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为移动机器人、机械臂、自动驾驶高层规划器时，常受恶意文本/语音/视觉指令等影响。现有防御多只判断动作是否物理安全，忽略了"物理安全但仍违反授权任务"的风险（如改道、替换目标物、扩大作业区域、启用无用传感器、延迟任务），即安全合规任务劫持。作者希望填补这一授权层面的防护空白。

Method: 提出MissionPAIR：自适应攻击框架，搜索能通过安全门但违反已认证任务的可执行计划。提出AuthGuard-R：确定性授权层，将每个可执行动作绑定到签名任务、机器人身份、物体与区域范围、当前状态、时间与输入来源，并与独立安全门组成双门架构。对机器人轨迹上的任务策略进行形式化，定义安全博弈，证明授权健全性、任务非升级、重放抵抗、机器人绑定、来源分离、阈值审批安全、审计日志防篡改、轨迹级组合等性质。

Result: 跨模型初步评估（Claude Haiku 4.5 与开源 Qwen2.5 7B 规划器）：240次实时攻击试验中，规划器有109次遵从注入的任务偏离；AuthGuard-R 全部拒绝了这109次由偏离产生的未授权动作。另有11个手工构造的协议与策略级攻击套件，也被完全阻断。

Conclusion: 物理安全验证不足以保障任务授权安全，任务劫持是现实威胁。AuthGuard-R 的确定性双门授权架构可在不引入物理风险的前提下阻塞此类攻击，形式化性质与初步实验均支持其有效性；未来需更大规模的模型与任务评测。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as high-level planners for mobile robots, robot manipulators, and autonomous vehicles. Recent studies show that these systems can be influenced through malicious text, speech, visual instructions, retrieved documents, and poisoned sensory context. Most defenses ask whether a proposed action is physically safe. This paper studies a different problem: an action may be physically safe and still violate the mission authorized by the user. An attacker may redirect a delivery robot, replace an approved object, extend a robot's operating region, activate an unnecessary sensor, or delay a mission without creating an immediate physical hazard. We call this attack \emph{safety-compliant mission hijacking}. We propose MissionPAIR, an adaptive attack framework that searches for executable plans that pass a safety gate while violating an authenticated mission. We also propose AuthGuard-R, a deterministic authorization layer that binds every executable action to a signed mission, robot identity, object and region scope, current state, time, and input provenance. AuthGuard-R operates with an independent safety gate, giving a dual-gate architecture. We formalize mission policies over robot traces, define security games, and prove authorization soundness, mission non-escalation, replay resistance, robot binding, provenance separation, threshold-approval security, audit-log tamper evidence, and trace-level composition. We report a preliminary cross-model evaluation with Claude Haiku~4.5 and the open-source Qwen2.5~7B planner. Across 240 live attack trials, the planners followed an injected mission deviation in 109 trials; AuthGuard-R rejected all 109 resulting unauthorized actions. A separate hand-constructed suite of eleven protocol- and policy-level attacks was also blocked completely.

</details>


### [143] [DualManip: Agentic Dynamic Manipulation via Dual-Path Semantic Reasoning and Geometric Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31112)
*Chengxi Li,Yan Di,Yingyue Li,Ruida Zhang,Mingyang Li,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DualManip 提出双路径框架，将低频语义推理与高频几何自适应解耦，以应对动态场景中的操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）虽支持开放词汇的机器人操作推理，但推理延迟高，难以满足动态场景的实时响应需求。许多场景变化只改变物体几何形状而不改变任务意图，因此无需每次都触发昂贵的语义重规划。

Method: 框架包含两条路径：语义路径负责任务分解与任务相关交互的落地，并通过约束求解模块进行位姿优化；几何路径基于实时 RGB-D 观测，用形状自适应网络持续更新模板与观测之间的对应关系，将任务相关的抓取接触跨观测迁移，从而在物体运动与非刚性形变下实现在线抓取重建。信息交互模块连接两路径，负责从语义落地初始化任务相关抓取、校验几何更新、并在更新失败时触发语义重规划。

Result: 在覆盖非刚性形变、关节重配置、刚性运动和高精度装配的六项真实世界操作任务、三种设置（静态、单次变化、连续动态）下评估。DualManip 表现出更强的操作鲁棒性，尤其在连续场景变化下，同时几何自适应用时比智能体式验证与语义重规划快约 46 倍。

Conclusion: 通过双路径解耦高频几何自适应与低频语义推理，可在保持任务意图的同时显著提升动态场景下的响应速度与操作鲁棒性，为实时开放词汇操作提供可行范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable open-vocabulary reasoning for robot manipulation, but their high inference latency limits responsiveness in dynamic scenes. Many scene changes, however, alter object geometry without invalidating task intent. We present DualManip, a dual-path framework that decouples infrequent semantic reasoning from responsive geometric adaptation. The semantic path decomposes the task and grounds task-relevant interactions, followed by a constraint-solving module for pose optimization. During execution, the geometric path continuously updates template-to-observation correspondences from live RGB-D observations via a shape-adaptive network. These correspondences transfer task-relevant grasp contacts across observations, enabling online grasp reconstruction under object motion and non-rigid deformation. The Information Interaction Module bridges the two paths by initializing task-relevant grasps from semantic grounding, validating geometric updates, and triggering semantic replanning upon update failures. Real-world evaluation spans six manipulation tasks covering non-rigid deformation, articulated reconfiguration, rigid motion, and high-precision assembly across three settings: static, single-change, and continuous dynamic. DualManip demonstrates superior manipulation robustness, particularly under continuous scene changes, while achieving geometric adaptation approximately 46$\times$ faster than agentic verification and semantic replanning. Our project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [144] [Comparative Evaluation of an XR Pen-based Control Interface for Semi-Autonomous Mobile Robot Navigation in Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31117)
*Alicia Torc,Carl Tornberg,Eric Piette,Renaud Ronsse,Benoit Macq,Gustavo Alfonso Garcia Ricardez,Lotfi El Hafi,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用商用XR手写笔在AR中控制半自主移动机器人：指定目标点并拖动增广箭头设定朝向；与XR手柄、手势及RViz基线对比（10人用户研究）。XR手写笔在任务选择时间上显著最优，XR手柄在感知工作负荷与可用性评分上最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境中服务机器人难以部署：一是全自主运行在不可预测环境中尚不可靠，二是传统控制界面对新手用户门槛高。XR虽能将机器人信息叠加于真实世界，但常见XR控制方式（运动手柄、手势）仍被认为不直观，因此需要探索更自然直观的XR控制交互范式（如XR笔）。

Method: 构建基于商用XR笔的AR控制界面：操作者在房间中指向位置、选中目标，并拖动增广箭头设置机器人到达目标处的期望朝向；在同一框架内另实现基于XR运动手柄与手势的两种界面。以家庭式环境中的导航任务进行10名被试的对照用户研究，比较四种控制方式（XR笔、XR运动手柄、手势、计算机端RViz基线），评估任务性能与主观感知。

Result: XR笔在任务选择时间上显著优于其他方法，且选择一致性最高；XR运动手柄获得最佳感知工作负荷与可用性评分，优于计算机基线，支持基于XR的控制作为面向新手用户的直观替代方案；但由于新发布的XR笔在集成上的技术限制，其用户体验目前受到制约。

Conclusion: XR笔在手势指定类导航交互的效率与一致性上占优，XR手柄在主观可用性上更佳，整体表明XR控制可作为新手用户的直观替代方案；未来需解决XR笔的集成技术限制，以释放其交互潜力。

Abstract: Service robots remain difficult to deploy in domestic environments, partly because fully autonomous operation is not yet reliable in unpredictable surroundings, and partly because conventional control methods remain inaccessible to novice users. Extended Reality (XR) enables operators to visualize robot information overlaid onto the real world and to interact with augmented elements. Yet, common XR control methods, such as motion controllers and hand gestures, are still perceived as unintuitive. This paper presents a control interface that uses a commercial XR pen to command a semi-autonomous mobile robot in Augmented Reality (AR): the operator points at a position in the room, selects it, and drags an augmented arrow to set the desired orientation of the robot at this destination. Two additional interfaces, based on the XR motion controllers and hand gestures, were developed within the same framework. To assess the performance and users' perception of these interfaces, and of the XR pen in particular, a study with 10 participants compared four control methods, i.e., the XR pen, the XR motion controllers, hand gestures, and a computer-based baseline RViz, in navigation tasks performed in a home-like environment. Results show that the XR pen significantly outperforms the other methods in task selection time with the most consistent selections, and that the XR motion controllers obtain the best perceived workload and usability scores, ahead of the computer-based baseline, supporting XR-based control as an intuitive alternative for novice users. However, technical limitations in the integration of the recently released XR pen currently hold back its user experience.

</details>


### [145] [INTERACT: Interactive Planning for Autonomous Driving via Anchor-Conditioned Prediction and Trust-Region Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31137)
*Aron Distelzweig,Andreas Look,Faris Janjoš,Steffen Hagedorn,Luigi Palmieri,Joschka Boedecker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: INTERACT 提出“锚点条件预测 + 信赖域 refinement”的交互式规划框架：将交互规划分解为跨驾驶意图的预测与意图内优化，用少量地图几何导出的锚点触发一次 ego-conditioned 预测，再用 CEM 在信赖域约束下连续精修，在 nuPlan 与 interPlan 闭环基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集城市交通中驾驶本质是交互式的：合并、无保护转向能否成功取决于周围智能体对自车的反应，而“先预测后规划”的传统范式无法建模这种双向依赖。已有的联合预测-规划方法要么联合训练导致任务干扰，要么分离但只能从预定义候选集中选取方案，缺乏连续性与效率。

Method: 核心洞察：周围智能体响应的是轨迹所表达的意图而非其精确实现，因此单次反应式预测可覆盖整个计划族。方法据此把交互规划分解为（1）跨驾驶意图的预测：从地图几何中导出少量多样化意图锚点，每个锚点查询一次专用的 ego-conditioned 预测模型；（2）意图内优化：用交叉熵方法（CEM）精修每个锚点，并施加信赖域惩罚，使精修计划足够接近锚点以保证条件化反应仍有效。预测保持独立模型以避免任务干扰，锚点并行处理，每个锚点仅需一次预测器查询而非每个候选计划一次。

Result: 在 nuPlan 与 interPlan 闭环基准上达到新的 SOTA，且增益最大的场景正是驱动该方法的交互式场景（合并、无保护转向等）。

Conclusion: INTERACT 通过锚点条件预测与信赖域精修，在保持预测-规划解耦（避免任务干扰）的同时恢复了交互依赖建模，且计算高效（每锚点一次查询、锚点并行），最终计划不受锚点集限制。代码将在接收后开源。

Abstract: Driving in dense urban traffic is interactive: whether a merge or an unprotected turn succeeds depends on how surrounding agents respond to the ego vehicle. Conventional planners predict first and plan second and, therefore, cannot account for this dependency. Methods that integrate prediction and planning either train both jointly, which introduces task interference, or keep them separate and are restricted to a predefined set of proposals. We present INTERACT: Interactive Planning for Autonomous Driving via Anchor-Conditioned Prediction and Trust-Region Refinement. Our key insight is that surrounding agents react to the intent a trajectory expresses rather than to its exact realization, so a single reactive prediction stays valid across an entire family of plans. INTERACT therefore decomposes interactive planning into prediction across driving intents and optimization within each intent. We derive a small set of diverse intents, which we call anchors, from map geometry, query a dedicated ego-conditioned prediction model once per anchor, and refine every anchor with the Cross-Entropy Method under a trust-region penalty that keeps the refined plan close enough to its anchor for the conditioned reaction to still apply. Prediction thus remains a separate model, avoiding task interference, while conditioning on anchors preserves the dependency. Because each anchor is refined continuously, the final plan is not restricted to the anchor set, yet INTERACT requires only one predictor query per anchor rather than one per candidate plan, with all anchors processed in parallel. On the nuPlan and interPlan closed-loop benchmarks, INTERACT sets a new state of the art, with the largest gains precisely in the interactive scenarios that motivate the method. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [146] [Evaluating the Impact of Adaptive Extended Reality on Human-Robot Interaction Across the Reality-Virtuality Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31138)
*Carl Tornberg,Alicia Torck,Lotfi El Hafi,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究XR交互模态（AR/AV/VR及动态RVC）对移动机械臂人机交互任务绩效的影响，发现固定模态影响结果，动态调节虚拟度可提升吞吐并降低负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发达国家人口老龄化与劳动力短缺促使以赛博化身扩展人类能力，需要有效HRI框架；XR可提供接口，但既有研究多固定XR模态，未评估模态对任务结果的影响，也未探索运行时自适应模态切换的价值。

Method: 构建与移动机械臂对接的自定义XR应用，支持沉浸式控制与运行时模态切换。采用被试内多房间抓放实验，对比固定AR、AV、VR与动态RVC条件，采集任务指标、NASA-TLX与SUS。

Result: 固定现实模态显著影响HRI结果；沿RVC动态改变模态可改善表现。AR在心理需求、努力与挫败感上显著低于AV与VR；动态RVC条件取得最高吞吐量与最低工作负荷。

Conclusion: 自适应XR界面有助于人机共生；模态选择与动态调节应作为HRI设计变量而非固定前提。代码已开源。

Abstract: As populations in developed countries age and labor shortages intensify, Cybernetic Avatars (CAs) are proposed to extend human capabilities through robotic embodiments, requiring effective Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) frameworks. Extended Reality (XR), an umbrella term for Augmented Reality (AR), Augmented Virtuality (AV), and Virtual Reality (VR), offers such interfaces, but prior research typically fixes the XR modality without evaluating its effect on task outcomes. This study examines whether the XR modality impacts HRI performance and whether an adaptive interface adjusting the level of virtuality along the Reality-Virtuality Continuum (RVC) at runtime improves it. A custom XR application interfaced with a mobile manipulator supports immersive control and runtime modality switching. In a within-participant multi-room pick-and-place experiment comparing fixed AR, AV, and VR with dynamic RVC through task metrics, the NASA-TLX, and the System Usability Scale (SUS), this study demonstrates that 1) the fixed reality modality affects HRI results, and 2) dynamically changing the modality along the RVC improves them. AR yielded significantly lower mental demand, effort, and frustration than AV and VR, while the dynamic RVC condition achieved the highest throughput and lowest workload, highlighting the value of adaptive XR interfaces for human-robot symbiosis. The implementation is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [147] [Onboard Wind-Preview Model Predictive Control Using Pitot-Static Sensing for Multirotor UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31185)
*Bas Meere,Eline Wisse,Laurens Vousten,Sander Doodeman,Elena Torta,Paula Chanfreut,Duarte Antunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在无人机前方空速管吊杆上预先测量风扰动，将风预览并入非线性MPC，实现前馈式阵风抑制与稳定悬停。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阵风抑制方法多为被动反应式：要么从运动响应反推扰动，要么在机体上测风，此时风已开始作用，补偿滞后。作者希望提前感知来风，实现前馈补偿，从而提升户外悬停精度。

Method: 在吊杆前端安装低成本、轻量的静压-总压（pitot-static）传感器，测量无人机前方来风，得到风预览信号；将其作为扰动预测输入非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC），在优化飞行轨迹时提前考虑风扰动。吊杆越长预览时间越长，但增大转动惯量、降低飞行性能，故在仿真中刻画该权衡，并分析最优预览距离随风速和无人机响应速度的变化。

Result: 仿真表明最优预览距离并非平台固有属性，而是随风速和响应速度偏移；室内硬件实验验证该趋势，控制器相对PX4基线和同等但风不可知的MPC显著提升悬停性能；户外实验沿风向误差相对基线降低54%。

Conclusion: 单个与风向对齐的低成本传感器即可提供有效风预览，配合NMPC显著改善抗风悬停，说明前馈式扰动预见是可行的低成本方案；吊杆长度需按工况动态权衡。

Abstract: Effective wind gust rejection and stable hovering are critical for the outdoor operation of autonomous drones. However, existing gust rejection methods are primarily reactive, inferring the disturbance from the resulting motion or measuring it at the airframe. Either way, the wind has already begun to act before it can be compensated. In this work, we anticipate the gust instead by measuring the wind ahead of the drone with a low-cost, low-weight pitot-static sensor mounted on a boom. The resulting wind preview is incorporated into a nonlinear model predictive controller (MPC), which optimizes the drone motion while anticipating wind disturbances. A longer boom offers more preview time but adds inertia and degrades flight performance. We characterize this trade-off in simulation and show that the optimal preview distance is not a fixed property of the platform, but shifts with the wind speed and with how quickly the drone can respond. Indoor hardware experiments confirm the trend and show that the proposed controller substantially improves hover performance against a PX4 baseline and an otherwise identical wind-unaware MPC. Outdoor experiments show that the error along the wind direction is reduced by 54 percent with respect to the baseline, demonstrating that a single wind-aligned sensor can significantly improve hovering performance.

</details>


### [148] [Enabling a Unified Cross-Domain Representation for Two-Finger Gripper Manipulation via Interaction-Centric Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31207)
*Guanlin Li,Shifeng Bao,Yihan Zhao,Haitao Shen,Haoyang Li,Chen Zhao,Tong Yang,Jie Tang,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以交互为中心的模仿学习框架：用参数化通用夹爪抽象构建规范夹爪坐标系表示，VLM 从语言与RGB-D推断子任务并定位交互三元组，SAM 2.1跟踪掩码减少VLM查询；结合目标/碰撞人工势场与夹爪坐标系点云的混合特征，用Flow-Matching Transformer预测7-DoF动作块，首次同时实现竞争力基准成绩与跨本体/跨视角零样本sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨本体泛化的核心障碍是表示缺陷：任务语义与硬件相关的视觉几何被纠缠在一起。两指夹爪虽形态各异（平行夹爪、吸盘等），却共享"夹持-交互"结构，因此需要一种与本体无关的规范表示来解耦语义与几何。

Method: 1) 参数化通用夹爪抽象，将各类两指夹爪映射到规范夹爪坐标系；2) VLM 依据语言与RGB-D推断子任务并定位交互三元组（gripper、held、target），SAM 2.1 进行掩码跟踪以降低VLM调用频率；3) 设计混合特征：目标/碰撞人工势场提供全局引导，夹爪坐标系分割点云提供局部几何；4) Flow-Matching Transformer 预测平滑的7-DoF动作序列块。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，该方法在基准测试上取得有竞争力分数，并首次实现面向完全不同、异构机器人平台的极端跨本体与跨视角零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 以交互为中心的规范夹爪表示可有效解耦任务语义与本体几何，是实现鲁棒跨本体模仿学习与零样本sim-to-real迁移的可行路径。局限在于依赖两指夹爪假设与VLM/分割模型的先验质量。

Abstract: Achieving robust cross-embodiment generalization in imitation learning demands overcoming a critical representation flaw that inextricably entangles task semantics with hardware-specific visual geometry. We propose an interaction-centric framework that leverages the shared structure of two-finger grippers via a parameterized universal gripper abstraction, yielding a canonical gripper-frame representation. Given language and RGB-D observations, a VLM infers the subtask and grounds an interaction triplet (gripper, held, target), while SAM~2.1 tracks masks to reduce VLM queries. We design concise hybrid features that combine target/collision artificial potential fields for global guidance with segmented gripper-frame point clouds for local geometry, and use a Flow-Matching Transformer to predict smooth 7-DoF action chunks. Experiments in simulation and real-world tasks demonstrate that ours is the first imitation learning approach to simultaneously achieve competitive benchmark scores and extreme cross-embodiment/cross-viewpoint zero-shot sim-to-real transfer to completely distinct, heterogeneous robot platforms.

</details>


### [149] [CoralPlan: Observation Skill Selection and Execution for Underwater Robotic Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31211)
*Yuer Gao,Yu Zhao,Yi Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoralPlan：视觉-语言系统从当前图像与任务文本中选择观测技能（orbit/patch/survey），经共享运动接口执行目标相对轨迹，并在144仿真与36对仿真-硬件实验中评估观测完成率与技能选择联合成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人巡检中，识别目标只是起点，机器人还需针对结构复杂的目标（如珊瑚群落）选择并执行合适的观测运动；如何将观测技能选择与可测量的执行结果关联，是任务导向采集的关键缺口。

Method: 构建视觉-语言技能选择系统，输入当前相机图像与episode manifest提供的任务文本，输出观测技能（orbit、patch、survey）及剩余计划字段作为操作者指引；通过共享运动接口将技能实例化为目标相对轨迹；定义观测完成（目标保持+技能特定的覆盖要求）与联合成功（选择与记录的参考技能一致且观测完成）两类指标。

Result: 在144个仿真episode和36对仿真-硬件配对中评估；清水水池、外部目标参考位姿条件下，硬件观测完成率77.8%，联合成功率63.9%；失败模式包括参考不匹配的完成与技能选择正确但观测不完整两类。

Conclusion: 结果将观测技能选择与可测量的水下执行结果联系起来，并定位了任务导向采集成功与失败的具体环节；该接口可作为水下巡检中技能级规划与真实硬件执行的评估范式。

Abstract: Underwater robotic inspection depends on acquiring views that reveal task-relevant structure. For a structurally complex coral colony, recognising the target is only the starting point: the robot must select and execute a viewing motion suited to the inspection task. We present CoralPlan, a vision-language system that selects an observation skill from a current camera image and task text supplied by an episode manifest. A shared motion interface executes orbit, patch, or survey as target-relative trajectories; the remaining plan fields provide operator guidance. Observation completion requires target keeping and primitive-specific coverage, while joint success also requires selection to match the recorded reference. We evaluate this interface in 144 simulated episodes and 36 matched simulation-hardware pairs. In a clear-water pool with external target-reference poses, hardware observation completion reaches 77.8% and joint success reaches 63.9%. The experiments identify both reference-mismatched completions and incomplete observations after a matching skill selection. These results connect observation-skill choice to measurable underwater execution outcomes and identify where task-directed acquisition succeeds or fails.

</details>


### [150] [Imp-ACT: Adaptive Impedance Control and Action Chunking with Transformers to Learn Contact-Rich Manipulation from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31225)
*Luca Zanetti,Doganay Sirintuna,Idil Ozdamar,Pietro Balatti,Heng Zhang,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Imp-ACT，在遥操作演示采集中引入方向依赖的笛卡尔刚度调制：自调节阻抗控制器沿瞬时运动方向自适应刚度，刚度作为动作标签与视觉/本体/力觉观测一同记录，由 ACT 预测位姿、夹爪与运动方向刚度。在擦除与插孔任务上以成功率与接触力指标评估，显著降低接触力振动与法向力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-动作策略通常把顺应性（阻抗/刚度）固定在控制器层面，无法随任务方向需求变化：沿任务执行方向需要足够刚度以保证跟踪精度，正交方向则需柔顺以限制接触力。已有方法依赖人工选刚度或离线目标重建，难以规模化采集"动作+顺应性"耦合的演示。

Method: 1) 遥操作阶段：自调节阻抗控制器沿瞬时运动方向自适应刚度，正交方向保持柔顺，无需人工调参；2) 将自适应刚度与视觉观测、运动指令一同记录，使动作与顺应性处于同一动力学下；3) 用 Action Chunking with Transformer (ACT) 从视觉、本体与力/力矩观测预测末端位姿、夹爪动作及运动方向刚度。

Result: 在擦除与插孔任务上，以成功率和接触行为定量指标评估。相比固定低/高刚度基线，Imp-ACT 成功率相当或更高且交互力更低：擦除任务接触力振动较柔顺基线降低约 29 倍、较刚性基线降低约 180 倍；插孔任务正交于插入方向的力较更优的固定刚度基线降低 43%。

Conclusion: 结果表明：沿任务执行所需方向保持足够刚度、同时在其余方向保持柔顺，可有效限制接触力并适应环境约束，说明将方向依赖刚度纳入演示采集与策略预测是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to balance accurate motion tracking with compliant interaction, yet most visual-action policies leave compliance fixed at the controller level. We present Imp-ACT, a methodologically grounded and practical approach to incorporating direction-dependent Cartesian stiffness modulation directly into demonstration collection, without manual stiffness selection or offline target reconstruction. During teleoperation, a self-tuning impedance controller adapts stiffness along the instantaneous direction of motion while maintaining compliance in orthogonal directions. The adapted stiffness is applied and recorded alongside visual observations and motion commands, capturing motion and compliance under the same dynamics. We implement this pipeline using Action Chunking with Transformer (ACT) to predict end-effector pose, gripper action, and motion-direction stiffness from visual, proprioceptive, and wrench observations. The performance of Imp-ACT is evaluated on wiping and plug insertion using both success rate and quantitative measures of contact behavior. Compared with fixed low- and high-stiffness baselines, Imp-ACT achieves comparable or higher success while maintaining low interaction forces. In wiping, it reduces contact-force vibration by approximately $29\times$ relative to the compliant baseline and $180\times$ relative to the stiff baseline. In plug insertion, it reduces forces orthogonal to the insertion direction by $43\%$ relative to the better fixed-stiffness baseline. These results highlight the benefit of maintaining sufficient stiffness along the direction needed for task execution while preserving compliance in other directions to limit contact forces and accommodate environmental constraints.

</details>


### [151] [Cybflight: An Embedded Rust Autopilot for Aerial Robotics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31232)
*Yifan Lin,Chao Qin,H S Helson Go,Hugh H.-T. Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cybflight：基于嵌入式Rust的模块化开源研究自驾仪，在单块STM32H743上无伴飞计算机运行MPCTC与INDI，室内达12.38 m/s、GNSS室外飞行达31.4 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从仿真迁移到实飞通常需重建成熟自驾仪或增设伴飞计算机，阻碍模块化研究方法的部署。作者希望证明：在飞控MCU上完全运行高负载状态估计与非线性控制，无需牺牲自驾仪的模块化结构。

Method: 提出Cybflight开源嵌入式Rust研究自驾仪，采用类型化、可替换接口连接硬件访问、感知、状态估计、轨迹规划与控制；以可替换的Rust实现模型预测轮廓跟踪控制（MPCTC）与增量非线性动态逆（INDI），单块STM32H743运行，无伴飞计算机。

Result: 室内飞行速度达12.38 m/s；使用GNSS位置更新的室外飞行达31.4 m/s。表明在飞控微控制器上运行高算力估计与非线性控制可与模块化自驾仪结构共存。

Conclusion: 类型化可替换接口加编译期优化的模块化开发流程，是嵌入式自驾仪承载先进估计与控制的有效路径，降低从仿真到实飞的迁移成本。

Abstract: Bringing an aerial robotics method from simulation to flight should not require rebuilding a mature autopilot or adding a companion computer. Cybflight is an open-source embedded Rust research autopilot whose typed, replaceable interfaces connect hardware access, perception, state estimation, trajectory planning, and control. This modular development and compile-time optimization workflow is demonstrated with replaceable Rust implementations of model predictive contour-tracking control (MPCTC) and incremental nonlinear dynamic inversion (INDI) running on one STM32H743 without a companion computer. Using this configuration, the vehicle reaches 12.38 m/s during indoor flight, while an outdoor flight using global navigation satellite system (GNSS) position updates reaches 31.4 m/s. These flights show that running demanding estimation and nonlinear control entirely on a flight-controller microcontroller need not come at the expense of a modular autopilot structure.

</details>


### [152] [Towards VLA-Dreamer: Refining VLA Behavior Using World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31313)
*Parsa Mastouri Kashani,Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在VLA视觉编码器嵌入空间上训练预测式世界模型的概念架构，以提升样本效率并支持基于目标图像的短时规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人控制上潜力大，但依赖海量高质量模仿学习数据，且缺乏显式世界模型，使控制能力受到质疑。作者希望验证VLA视觉嵌入是否具备动作相关性并可用于未来预测——若不能预测，则说明VLA架构缺少无损的隐式世界模型。

Method: 概念性（concept paper）架构设计：在VLA视觉编码器的嵌入空间上训练世界模型动力学，损失定义在嵌入空间而非像素空间，思路类似JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）。训练后的世界模型可通过给定目标图像采样VLA动作，用于短时规划。属于假设驱动的提案，未给出具体实验。

Result: 无实验结果。论文仅为研究计划/概念提案，提出待验证的假设与实验设想，未提供定量或实机验证。

Conclusion: 若能证明VLA视觉嵌入可被动作条件化地预测，则可在推理阶段生成规划并降低数据需求；反之则暴露VLA缺乏隐式世界模型这一关键局限。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs), while showing strong potential for robot control, require massive amounts of high-quality imitation learning data. Moreover, the absence of an explicit world model casts further doubt on their control capabilities. In this concept paper, we propose a novel architecture that addresses sample efficiency in VLAs by training a predictive world model on the embedding space of the VLA's vision encoder. We hypothesize that these embeddings are action-relevant and usable for future prediction. To this end, we propose using the suggested architecture to investigate how well these embeddings predict the future based on actions, as the inability to do so would mark a key limitation of VLA architectures: the lack of a non-lossy implicit world model to simulate real-world dynamics. The proposed architecture differs from the standard world model dynamics as the loss comes from the embedding space rather than the pixel space, similar to joint embedding predictive architectures. Furthermore, the trained world model can be utilized for short-term planning tasks by sampling VLA actions given goal images. We intend to examine the richness of vision embeddings in VLAs and reduce their high data requirements through a world model that can also generate plans during inference.

</details>


### [153] [See to Reach, Feel to Grasp: Learning A Blind Grasp Reflex for Anthropomorphic Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31323)
*Alexander Alexiev,Tzu-Yuan Lin,Sang Min Kim,Ho Jae Lee,Yonghyeon Lee,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化灵巧抓取架构，将手臂全局运动与手部局部接触控制解耦，仅用本体感觉实现无视觉盲抓，并用学习到的稳定抓取分数触发抓后操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉抓取依赖图像、物体位姿与几何观测，在遮挡、低光照或传感受限场景下易失效；同时抓取与手臂控制耦合，难以复用。作者希望验证仅凭手部本体感觉即可稳健抓取多样物体，并将抓取抽象为可复用的手级技能。

Method: 模块化架构：独立控制的手臂将手引导至物体附近，强化学习策略仅以手部本体感觉为输入完成接触、抓取与稳定；训练一个稳定抓取分数（learned stable-grasp score）判断物体是否被牢固握持，作为抓后操作的切换信号。盲抓不依赖图像、物体位姿或几何观测。

Result: 在仿真与真实硬件上验证了对多样物体的稳健盲抓，并能与多种独立设计的手臂控制器无缝组合；学习到的抓取分数与独立的物理稳定性度量高度一致，尽管从未观测接触几何。

Conclusion: 遵循"see to reach, feel to grasp"原则，视觉与触觉/本体感觉解耦，抓取成为可复用的手级技能，可与不同手臂控制器组合用于多样化操作任务。

Abstract: In this work we study if a robotic hand using proprioception alone can grasp diverse objects with no visual observation. We present a modular dexterous grasping architecture that separates global arm motion from local contact control. An independently controlled arm guides the hand toward the object, while a reinforcement learning policy grasps and stabilizes it using only hand proprioceptive feedback. We call this \textit{a blind grasp reflex}: grasping without images, object poses, or geometric observations. A learned stable-grasp score determines when the object is securely held, allowing the arm to begin post-grasp manipulation. This separation makes grasping a reusable hand-level skill that can be combined with independently designed arm controllers for various manipulation tasks. Experiments in simulation and on hardware demonstrate robust blind grasping across diverse objects and seamless composition with a range of arm controllers. Moreover, despite never observing contact geometry, the learned grasp score closely aligns with an independent physics-based measure of grasp stability. The resulting approach follows a simple principle: see to reach, feel to grasp. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [154] [Representation-Guided Generation and Integration of Executable Programs for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31337)
*Ruixiao Yang,Mingxin Yu,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RIVET 提出以物体为中心的共享表征（逐物体 6D 位姿 + 关系图）引导 VLM 生成感知、渲染、关系推理与规划程序，从而把独立生成的模块集成为可复用、可跨初始/目标配置复用的完整操作任务系统，在仿真与真机的堆叠、七巧板重排、三维装配任务上达到真机 83% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建机器人操作系统需通过精心设计的表征与接口连接感知、规划和控件；VLM 代码生成虽能自动化构建，但各组件独立生成时可能依赖不兼容的几何与任务级信息，导致无法真正集成。作者希望用一个共享的物体中心表征作为"接口契约"，使生成出的模块在几何度量与任务结构层面保持一致。

Method: 核心是物体中心表征：每个物体的 6D 位姿（保留动作落地所需的度量信息）＋关系图（暴露规划所需的任务级结构）。在该表征引导下，VLM 生成四类协同程序——感知、渲染、关系推理、规划——每类结合任务特定计算与可用软件包。程序按操作域一次性生成，之后在未见过的初始与目标配置上复用，无需重新生成代码。评估任务为立方体堆叠、七巧板重排、三维装配，覆盖不同几何、关系与顺序要求，并在仿真与物理机器人上验证。

Result: 在仿真与真机上均完成任务；通过复用离线生成的系统，真机总体成功率达 83%。结果表明表征引导的程序生成能让同一通用操作框架适配几何、关系与顺序需求各异的任务。

Conclusion: 共享的物体中心表征（6D 位姿＋关系图）可作为 VLM 生成模块间的有效接口，实现"一次生成、多配置复用"的完整操作系统；该方法提供了可跨任务族迁移的通用操作框架范式。

Abstract: Building a robotic manipulation system requires connecting perception, planning, and control through carefully designed representations and interfaces. VLM code generation offers a way to automate this construction, but independently generated components may operate on incompatible geometric and task-level information. We present Representation-guided Integration of VLM-generated Executable Task programs (RIVET), a framework for generating complete manipulation systems around a shared object-centric representation. The representation combines per-object 6D poses, which preserve the metric information required for action grounding, with a relation graph that exposes the task-level structure required for planning. Guided by this representation, a VLM generates cooperating perception, rendering, relation-inference, and planning programs, each combining task-specific computation with available packages where useful. The resulting programs are authored once for a manipulation domain and reused on unseen start and goal configurations without code regeneration. We evaluate RIVET on cube stacking, tangram rearrangement, and three-dimensional assembly in simulation and on a physical robot, where we achieve 83% overall success rate in the real world by reusing offline-generated systems. Our results demonstrate that representation-guided program generation can adapt a common manipulation framework to tasks with different geometric, relational, and sequential requirements.

</details>


### [155] [Transformer-based Monte Carlo Localization in Construction Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31357)
*Linus Kramer,William Talbot,Olga Vysotska,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LiDAR的全局重定位系统，将PointNet++编码器与位置识别解码器结合，作为MCL的学习型观测模型，仅用建筑网格仿真生成的合成LiDAR训练，在真实数据集上优于扩散与ScanContext++基线，单次推理18ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工现场移动机器人执行巡检或数字化任务时，需要相对建筑地图的全局参考系可靠定位；但相似房间布局与低纹理表面对现有LiDAR与视觉定位方法构成挑战，且真实标注数据获取成本高。

Method: 构建LiDAR全局重定位流水线：PointNet++编码器 + 位置识别解码器，输出作为MCL中的学习型观测模型；训练数据完全来自在建筑网格内仿真机器人传感器得到的合成LiDAR扫描。引入不确定性感知解码器对位置似然进行缩放，并采用重采样策略将模型假设注入粒子集，以缓解粒子耗尽问题。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上，本方法优于基于扩散与ScanContext++的基线，同时保持快速推理（每次调用18 ms），验证了合成数据训练在建筑机器人网格参考全局定位中的实用性。

Conclusion: 合成数据训练的LiDAR全局重定位可在模糊、低纹理的建筑环境中鲁棒工作，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: To be able to perform inspection or digitization tasks, mobile robots on construction sites must be able to localize themselves reliably with respect to a global reference frame that is shared with a building map. Similar room layouts and low-texture surfaces pose a challenge for existing LiDAR- and vision-based localization methods. We approach this problem with a LiDAR-based global relocalization system that estimates the robot's pose relative to a building mesh and combines a PointNet++ encoder with a place recognition decoder, whose outputs serve as a learned observation model within a Monte Carlo Localization (MCL) framework. The pipeline is trained exclusively on synthetic LiDAR scans obtained by simulating the robot's sensors inside the building mesh. Our approach is robust in ambiguous environments due to an uncertainty-aware decoder that scales positional likelihoods and a resampling strategy that injects model hypotheses into the particle set, enabling recovery from potential particle depletion. Evaluations on real-world datasets show that our method outperforms both diffusion-based and ScanContext++ baselines while maintaining fast inference (18 ms per call), demonstrating the practicality of synthetic-data training for mesh-referenced global localization in construction robotics.

</details>


### [156] [RECAST: From Log Replay to Closed-Loop Driving Simulation with View-Complete Actors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31374)
*Zijun Zhao,Liewen Liao,Kang Shen,Songan Zhang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RECAST 是一个基于 3D 高斯泼溅的驾驶闭环仿真框架：从驾驶日志中单帧分割车辆观测重建视角完备的可控演员，并注册到重建场景中，支持 planner-in-the-loop 渲染；配套发布约 2 万真实车辆、60 万张无背景 RGBA 图像的 RECAR 数据集与两阶段适配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环驾驶仿真要求 ego 与周围演员超出记录轨迹运动时仍能生成可靠观测，这些视角在源日志中缺失。现有数据驱动仿真器从稀疏观测重建动态演员，在视角变化下易产生渲染伪影，难以支撑可控交互下的规划器评估。

Method: 以 3D Gaussian Splatting 为表示，从驾驶日志中单次分割的车辆观测生成视角完备演员并配准到重建场景；为把图像到 3D 先验适配到真实车辆，构建 RECAR 数据集（约 2 万真实车辆、60 万无背景 RGBA 图像，覆盖多样颜色与车型），并采用两阶段适配（先合成数据、再真实日志观测）提升生成质量。

Result: 演员级 \(\mathrm{FD}_{\mathrm{incep}}\) 从未适配 TRELLIS 的 9.788 降至 7.992；场景级在超出日志轨迹的运动下，相对 Street Gaussians 将 \(\mathrm{FD}_{\mathrm{incep}}\) 从 129.35 降至 112.10，\(\mathrm{CLIP}_{\mathrm{margin}}\)(×1000) 从 0.14 提升至 3.47。与 GTRS-Dense 规划器联合的闭环评测中，相对原生 Street Gaussians 演员，无碰撞率 NC 从 22.2%(12/54) 提升到 63.0%(34/54)，平均最小预测 TTC 从 0.798 s 提升至 2.150 s。

Conclusion: RECAST 表明：借助视角完备的可控演员生成与两阶段真实数据适配，3DGS 仿真器可支撑超出日志回放、受控 ego-actor 交互下的闭环规划器评估，且渲染保真度与安全性指标均有实质提升。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving simulation requires rendered observations to remain reliable as the ego vehicle and surrounding actors move beyond their recorded trajectories, exposing views absent from the source log. Existing data-driven simulators reconstruct dynamic actors from sparse observations, which can result in rendering artifacts under these viewpoint changes. We introduce RECAST (REconstructing Controllable Actors for Simulation and Testing), a 3D Gaussian Splatting framework that generates a view-complete actor from a single segmented vehicle observation in a driving log and registers the generated actor in the reconstructed scene. RECAST supports planner-in-the-loop rendering under controlled ego-actor interactions. To adapt an image-to-3D prior to real vehicles, we further introduce RECAR, a dataset of approximately 20K real vehicles with 600K background-free RGBA images spanning diverse vehicle colors and types. We use two-stage adaptation to improve vehicle generation from real driving-log observations. At the actor level, RECAST reduces $\mathrm{FD}_{\mathrm{incep}}$ from 9.788 to 7.992 relative to unadapted TRELLIS. At the scene level, under actor motion beyond logged trajectories, RECAST reduces $\mathrm{FD}_{\mathrm{incep}}$ from 129.35 to 112.10 and increases $\mathrm{CLIP}_{\mathrm{margin}}$ ($\times1000$) from 0.14 to 3.47 relative to Street Gaussians. We demonstrate planner-in-the-loop simulation with the image-conditioned planner GTRS-Dense. Compared with native Street Gaussians actors, RECAST increases the no-collision (NC) rate from 22.2% (12/54) to 63.0% (34/54) and the mean minimum predicted time-to-collision (TTC) from 0.798 s to 2.150 s. These experiments show that RECAST supports closed-loop planner evaluation under controlled ego-actor interactions beyond log replay. Visit our project page at this https URL

</details>


### [157] [Guiding End-to-End Driving Models with Endpoint-Constrained Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31383)
*Brayden Zhang,Mahsa Golchoubian,Igor Gilitschenski,Boris Ivanovic,Kashyap Chitta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ECO后处理层，通过锚定执行历史、保留预测终点、重整形中间路点几何，缓解端到端驾驶开环训练与闭环部署的不匹配，在HUGSIM与AlpaSim上显著提升多种策略的闭环分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶策略常用开环行为克隆训练，但部署时必须闭环执行，存在训练-执行范式错配。除已广泛研究的协变量偏移与因果混淆外，作者指出一个补充因素：基于路点的监督与位移指标并不保证中间轨迹在物理上自洽或易于控制器跟踪；不一致主要集中在中间路点，而预测终点相对可靠。

Method: ECO（Endpoint-Constrained Optimization）：一个轻量后处理层，插在发出路点的策略与其控制器之间。它把轨迹锚定到车辆已执行的历史，保留策略预测的终点，并重整形中间路点以提升可行性。无需地图、无需特权仿真状态、无需额外训练，可跨多种生成式与回归式策略通用。

Result: 在两个闭环仿真器上，ECO提升了全部六个被测生成式与回归式驾驶策略的聚合闭环分数；基础规划违反运动学限制越频繁，增益越大。HUGSIM上VaVAM的HD-Score从18.1提升到31.0（+71%），获HUGSIM闭环驾驶挑战赛第1名；AlpaSim上VaVAM与DiffusionDrive场景分数分别提升123%与22%。

Conclusion: 对广泛的端到端驾驶模型而言，在不改变策略预测终点的前提下修复预测轨迹的中间几何，可实质性提升闭环性能，说明中间轨迹物理一致性是开环/闭环差距的一个可独立处理的关键因素。

Abstract: End-to-end driving policies are commonly trained through open-loop behavior cloning, yet they must ultimately operate in closed-loop when deployed on a vehicle, creating a fundamental mismatch between training and execution. Beyond the commonly studied effects of covariate shift and causal confusion, we identify a complementary factor for this open-loop/closed-loop gap: waypoint-based supervision and displacement metrics do not ensure that the intermediate trajectory is physically coherent or easy for the controller to track. We observe that these inconsistencies concentrate primarily at intermediate waypoints, while the predicted endpoint remains comparatively reliable. Based on this observation, we introduce Endpoint-Constrained Optimization (ECO), a lightweight postprocessing layer that anchors the trajectory to the vehicle's executed history, preserves the policy's predicted endpoint, and reshapes the intermediate waypoints to improve feasibility. ECO requires no map, privileged simulator state, or additional training, and can be inserted between a broad range of waypoint-emitting policies and their controllers. Across two closed-loop simulators, it improves the aggregate closed-loop score of all six evaluated generative and regression-based driving policies, and the gains tend to increase with how often the base plans violate motion limits. On HUGSIM, ECO improves VaVAM from 18.1 to 31.0 HD-Score (+71%), achieving 1st place on the HUGSIM Closed-Loop Driving Challenge. Similarly, on AlpaSim, ECO increases the scene scores of VaVAM and DiffusionDrive by 123% and 22%, respectively. These results show that for a broad collection of end-to-end driving models, repairing the intermediate geometry of predicted trajectories without changing the policy's predicted endpoint can substantially improve closed-loop performance.

</details>


### [158] [Modeling and Generative-AI-Based Design of Load-Adaptive Gravity Balancing Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31386)
*Ryotaro Kayawake,Kazuki Abe,Shota Miyake,Masahiro Watanabe,Kenjiro Tadakuma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与具体机构构型无关的负载自适应重力平衡机构（LA-GBM）通用设计方法：先推导其势场必须满足的必要条件，得到仿射形式与因式分解形式两种一般表达，再将其作为设计要求输入生成式AI产生候选势函数，并解析验证候选函数，最后将势函数分解为单项并用弹簧、配重与函数生成连杆机构加以组合实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负载自适应重力平衡机构可被动响应负载变化以适应多种工况，但设计困难：必须同时满足期望的机构运动与变载荷下的静态平衡。已有设计方法多依赖特定机构构型或特定机械元件，缺乏通用性。

Method: 1) 推导LA-GBM势场的必要条件，导出两种一般形式：表征负载质量效应的仿射形式，以及表征负载自适应与重力平衡状态切换的因式分解形式；2) 将上述形式作为设计要求提供给生成式AI，生成候选势函数；3) 对生成函数进行解析验证，检查其是否满足两种一般形式及有效LA-GBM所需条件；4) 将所得势函数分解为单项，给出以弹簧、配重和函数生成连杆机构组合构建LA-GBM的示例方法。以势函数作为中间表示，实现无需预先指定机构构型的候选设计生成。

Result: 给出了LA-GBM势场必要条件的两种一般形式（仿射形式与因式分解形式），并用其驱动生成式AI产出候选势函数；候选函数通过解析方式验证了与两种形式的符合性及有效性条件；同时给出将势函数分解并映射为弹簧、配重与连杆机构组合的构造示例。文中未报告物理样机实验或实测性能数据，机械可实现性与可制造性被列为未来工作。

Conclusion: 以势函数为中间表示，所提框架可在不预设机构构型的前提下生成LA-GBM设计候选，为变负载重力平衡机构提供通用设计流程；机械可实现性与可制造性仍是待解决的开放问题。

Abstract: Load-adaptive gravity balancing mechanisms (LA-GBMs) can accommodate various loading conditions by passively changing their characteristics in response to payload variations. However, their design is difficult because both the desired mechanism motion and static equilibrium under variable payloads must be satisfied simultaneously. This study proposes a general design methodology for LA-GBMs that does not depend on specific mechanism architectures or mechanical elements. The necessary conditions for the potential fields of LA-GBMs are formulated, and two general forms are derived: an affine form representing the effect of payload mass and a factorized form representing state transitions associated with load adaptation and gravity balancing. These forms are then provided to generative AI as design requirements to generate candidate potential functions. The generated functions are analytically verified in terms of their conformity to the two general forms and the conditions required for valid LA-GBMs. Furthermore, the obtained potential functions are decomposed into individual terms, and an example of a method for constructing an LA-GBM by combining springs, counterweights, and function-generating linkage mechanisms is presented. By using potential functions as an intermediate representation, the proposed framework enables the generation of LA-GBM design candidates without prescribing a mechanism architecture in advance. Mechanical realizability and manufacturability of the generated potential fields remain important issues for future work.

</details>


### [159] [InternW0-$Δ$: A World Action Model Bridging Predictive Dynamics and Actions with 20K+ Hours of Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31394)
*Xingyu Miao,Zizun Li,Baole Fang,Kaiwen Song,Tenghui Wang,Hanxue Zhang,Yating Wang,Xudong Li,Yuping He,Xueyuan Wei,Chao Gao,Xijie Yang,Yingxiang Xu,Kerui Ren,Wenqi Guo,Jianjun Zhou,Xinzhe Wang,Weiguang Zhao,Ni Yang,Zetao Cai,Yufei Xue,Hengjie Li,Zeyu He,Yuanzhen Zhou,Rong Fu,Jianyang Zhang,Siwei Cui,Fuxian Huang,Yunsong Zhou,Xing Gao,Yifei Yao,Qiaojun Yu,Kailin Li,Ming Zhou,Mu Huang,Xinyue Li,Wenze Cui,Bingqi Jiang,Xueyue Zhu,Junting Dong,Haoyu Guo,Tao Lu,Mulin Yu,Bowen Zhou,Bin Zhao,Tianfan Xue,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InternW0-Δ 是一个统一的世界动作模型（WAM），在包含机器人示教、UMI、第一视角人类示教与 Ego2Robot 数据的 20K+ 小时异构语料上预训练，通过 Mixture-of-Transformers 融合视频动力学、冻结 VLM 语义指导、4D 几何/运动先验（训练期蒸馏）与 Causal Imprint 未来表征，实现仿真与真机上的强操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略难以把大规模预训练模型中的多种先验（视觉动力学、场景语义、几何、运动）统一到同一个动作生成框架中；同时通用 WAM 训练缺乏大规模、跨形态对齐的异构数据语料。

Method: 提出 InternW0-Δ：基于 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构，视频专家与动作专家在冻结 VLM 语义引导下交互；预训练 4D 基础模型通过仅训练期蒸馏注入几何与运动先验；引入 Causal Imprint，用训练期未来监督学习与未来相关的场景变化，为动作专家提供预测性表征，推理时无需 rollout 未来视频。数据侧构建机器人示教、UMI、第一视角人类示教与 Ego2Robot 数据并在统一状态-动作表示下对齐。

Result: 构建超过 20K 小时已处理训练数据的开源语料（作者称同类中最大），并宣称在多个仿真基准与真机平台上优于先前方法。摘要未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一条将多源预训练先验统一进世界动作模型的可行路径，并以大规模异构语料支撑通用操作策略预训练；承诺开源训练代码、权重、基础设施、数据处理管线与可用许可内的数据。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly model visual dynamics and action generation for generalist robot manipulation. A central challenge is to integrate priors from large-scale pretrained models---including visual dynamics, scene semantics, geometry, and motion---into a unified framework for robot action generation. We introduce InternW0-$\Delta$, a unified WAM pretrained on a heterogeneous corpus that outperforms prior methods across simulation benchmarks and real-robot platforms. InternW0-$\Delta$ combines pretrained visual dynamics, scene-level semantics, 4D geometric and motion priors, and action generation within a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) framework. A pretrained video expert and an action expert interact under semantic guidance from a frozen VLM, while a pretrained 4D foundation model injects geometric and motion priors through training-only distillation. We further introduce Causal Imprint, which learns future-relevant scene changes from training-only future supervision and provides predictive representations directly to the action expert without future-video rollout at inference. For large-scale joint training, we construct a heterogeneous corpus of robot demonstrations, UMI data, egocentric human demonstrations, and Ego2Robot data, curated and aligned under a common state-action representation. The resulting corpus contains over 20K hours of processed training data, to our knowledge the largest open-source corpus of its kind. We pretrain InternW0-$\Delta$ on this corpus and demonstrate strong performance across simulation benchmarks and real-robot platforms. We will open source the training code, model weights, infrastructure, data-processing pipeline, and processed data where licenses permit. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [160] [Augmented Reality Interfaces for Human-Robot Collaboration: Development of a ROS 2-Based Sensor Streaming Framework and Validation via SLAM Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31396)
*Alessandro Rubert,Stefano Ghidoni,Matteo Terreran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一套连接 Magic Leap 2 AR 头显与 ROS 2 的传感器流传输框架，通过 Unity 与 ROSTCP-Connector 将位姿、相机与环境传感器数据实时发布为 ROS 2 话题，并用 SLAM 算法验证其精度、延迟与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业 4.0 与人机协作（HRC）需要双向、直观、高效的通信通道；AR 既能向操作者呈现信息，又能采集环境空间数据，因此需要一套把 AR 头显传感数据接入机器人软件栈的可靠数据通道。

Method: 基于 Unity 与 ROSTCP-Connector 为 Magic Leap 2 开发板载应用，实时采集位姿跟踪、相机与环境传感器数据，并发布到专用 ROS 2 话题；随后用文献中已有的 SLAM 算法对该数据流的精度、延迟与鲁棒性进行验证测试。

Result: 实验结果表明该架构可实现稳定的数据传输，为安全实时交互与共享空间感知奠定基础，并为复杂 HRC 场景下机器人系统的控制与监督开辟了新视角。摘要未给出量化指标。

Conclusion: 该工作属于系统集成与工程框架类贡献，验证了 AR 头显到 ROS 2 的传感流可行性，但缺乏量化评测与真实机器人协作任务的端到端演示，属相邻支撑性工作。

Abstract: In recent years, Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) has taken on a central role in Industry 4.0 and collaborative robotics, demanding communication channels that are increasingly bidirectional, intuitive, and efficient. In this context, Augmented Reality (AR) presents itself as a fundamental enabling technology, capable of both displaying information to the operator and gathering spatial data about the surrounding environment. This thesis presents the development of a sensor streaming framework that connects the Magic Leap 2 AR headset with the ROS 2 (Robot Operating System) ecosystem. Using the Unity development environment and the ROSTCP-Connector package, an on-board application for the headset was developed, capable of acquiring real-time data from the integrated sensors (pose tracking, cameras, and environmental sensors) and publishing it to dedicated ROS 2 topics. In order to test the accuracy, latency, and robustness of the generated data stream, the framework was validated using SLAM (Simultaneous Localization and Mapping) algorithms known in the literature. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed architecture ensures stable data transmission, laying the groundwork for safe real-time interaction and shared spatial awareness, and opening up new perspectives for the control and supervision of robotic systems in complex HRC scenarios.

</details>


### [161] [dRVG: Quadtree-Guided, Resolution-Complete Online Motion Planning for Polygonal Robots in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31412)
*Duo Zhang,Hechen Zhang,Junshan Huang,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动态旋转堆叠可见性图(dRVG)在线运动规划器，融合连续观测的局部路线图，规划多边形机器人无碰撞平移与旋转；用四叉树调度感知目标，实验在20张地图×7种角分辨率共140例全部求解，360层中位规划1.18s，并有microMVP实机在线闭环验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知静态环境中多边形机器人的在线运动规划通常依赖均匀位姿网格或完整地图，前者分辨率受限，后者在未知环境下不可得。作者希望在不使用均匀位置网格的前提下，同时处理平移与旋转，并降低重复感知访问的开销。

Method: 构建dRVG（dynamic rotation-stacked visibility graph），将连续观测得到的局部roadmap在旋转层上堆叠合并，从而在无均匀位置网格条件下规划无碰撞的平移+旋转。用空间quadtree调度各区域的感知目标以减少重复访问，同时保留全部朝向配置用于路由。在精确感知与几何计算、以及机器人/包络为星形（star-shaped）的假设下，证明了采用中心扫描时dRVG相对于同角分辨率的全图RVG具有resolution-complete性质。实验采用footprint扫描。

Result: 在20张困难地图、7种角分辨率共140个测试例中，20s预算内全部求解；360个朝向层时中位规划时间1.18s。另有6组microMVP演示验证了物理机器人上的完整在线规划闭环。

Conclusion: dRVG提供了一种无需均匀位置网格、兼顾平移与旋转的在线规划方案，具备相对全图RVG的分辨率完备性，且在困难地图集合上稳定快速求解并完成实机在线闭环。

Abstract: We present the dynamic rotation-stacked visibility graph (dRVG), an online motion planner that guides polygonal robots to specified goals in initially unknown, static environ- ments. It merges local roadmaps from successive observations to plan collision-free translations and rotations without a uniform position grid. A spatial quadtree schedules sensing goals across regions to reduce repeated visits while retaining all orientation configurations for routing. Under exact sensing and geometric computation and star-shaped robot and envelope assumptions, dRVG with center scans is resolution-complete relative to full- map RVG at the same angular resolution. In experiments using footprint scans, dRVG solves all 140 cases across 20 difficult maps and seven angular resolutions within a 20 s planning budget, with a median planning time of 1.18 s at 360 orientation layers. Six microMVP demonstrations illustrate the complete online planning loop on a physical robot.

</details>


### [162] [CognitiveReality: Robot-Agnostic Semantic Gaussian Mapping with an LLM Agent for Immersive Collaborative VR Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31418)
*Timofei Kozlov,Dmitrii Maliukov,Andrey Marchenko,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CognitiveReality 将机器人 RGB-D 流实时构建为语义索引的 Gaussian-TSDF 地图，供 VR 操作员与工具调用型语言智能体共享，实现开放词汇实例级场景理解、语音/指向接地与可确认的机器人动作，并在两只四足机器人上完成导航与重观测任务的实机部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯逼真 3D 视图只能告诉遥操作者机器人在哪，却无法说明场景包含什么、各物体被观测得多好，也无法把指向与语音转化为机器人动作。因此需要一个统一的地图表示，同时服务于 VR 中的操作员和具备工具调用能力的语言智能体，弥合感知、语义与动作之间的鸿沟。

Method: 单一 mapper 二进制仅靠配置适配多平台：接收来自机器人 SLAM、关节运动学、动捕或在线视觉跟踪器的位姿；用 shadow tracker 与关键帧锚定 PnP 桥接定位中断；以 2 Hz 维护开放词汇实例身份及逐物体质量。语音与控制器射线通过经过验证的类型化工具接地到持久场景物体，并经操作员确认后执行机器人动作。评估包含本地 Qwen3-VL-8B 路由器的受控智能体测试、合并感知回放，以及与 Gaussian+SDF 基线的对比。

Result: 受控智能体评估中，本地部署的 Qwen3-VL-8B 路由器达到 81.24% 工具精确匹配，合并感知回放正确重定向 101 个被吸收的物体标识符。在机器人数据上，CognitiveReality 较 Gaussian+SDF 基线提升 2–8 dB；在 5–40 秒 SLAM 中断期间位姿误差保持在 1–8 cm。在两台四足机器人上实测，智能体完成 30 次导航请求中的 26 次、20 次重观测请求中的 20 次，物体质量提升 2–5 dB。

Conclusion: 该工作把语义索引的 Gaussian-TSDF 地图、开放词汇实例质量与语言智能体工具调用统一在同一实时系统中，通过定位中断桥接与合并感知的标识符一致性保证了鲁棒性，并在真实四足平台上验证了导航与重观测任务的可执行性，为操作员与语言智能体共享的场景表示提供了可行范式。

Abstract: A photorealistic 3D view tells a teleoperator where a robot is, but not what the scene contains, how well each object has been observed, or how to turn pointing and speech into robot action. CognitiveReality turns a robot's RGB-D stream into a live, semantically indexed Gaussian-TSDF map shared by an operator in virtual reality and a tool-using language agent. One mapper binary serves any platform through configuration alone: it ingests poses from robot SLAM, joint kinematics, motion capture or an inline visual tracker, bridges localization outages with a shadow tracker and keyframe-anchored PnP, and maintains open-vocabulary instance identities with per-object quality at 2 Hz. Speech and controller rays are grounded against persistent scene objects through validated typed tools and operator-confirmed robot actions. In the controlled agent evaluation, the deployed local Qwen3-VL-8B router reaches 81.24\% tool exact match, while merge-aware replay correctly redirects 101 absorbed object identifiers. On robot data CognitiveReality exceeds a Gaussian-plus-SDF baseline by 2-8 dB; pose error through 5-40 s SLAM outages stays within 1-8 cm. Deployed live on two quadrupeds, the agent executed 26 of 30 navigation requests and 20 of 20 re-observation requests, raising object quality by 2-5 dB.

</details>


### [163] [ExoLaN: Physics-Consistent Context-Aware Dynamics Learning for Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31434)
*Lucas Schulze,Maximilian Schwarz,Jona Hoppe,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ExoLaN——一种上下文感知的 DeLaN 模型，融合时间上下文与鞋垫接触力测量，学习人-外骨骼耦合系统动力学，实现任务无关的关节力矩估计与正向预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务无关的外骨骼辅助控制需要估计人体关节力矩；现有物理一致方法（如 DeLaN）无法在不重训练的情况下适配特定用户，也未考虑行走中的间歇性接触。

Method: ExoLaN 在 DeLaN 基础上引入时间上下文与力敏鞋垫的部分接触力测量，推断潜在动力学嵌入并估计广义接触力矩；训练时采用多步预测损失以统一逆动力学与正向预测。

Result: 在 7 名未见用户、21 项未见任务上，力矩估计 MSE 相比黑盒基线降低 7%；多步损失使加速度 MSE 降低 59%，长时程位置与速度误差分别降低 60% 与 93%；潜在上下文无需任务标签即可捕获任务信息。

Conclusion: ExoLaN 提供物理一致、可适配上下文的人-外骨骼动力学统一模型，兼具逆动力学估计与正向预测能力，其潜在上下文可作为任务感知辅助控制的有效信号。

Abstract: Task-agnostic assistive exoskeleton control based on human intention offers greater flexibility than conventional approaches that rely on predefined tasks or motion patterns. Human joint torque estimation enables task-agnostic assistance by characterizing user actions. Physics-consistent methods such as Deep Lagrangian Networks (DeLaN) have been applied to estimate the human torques in multi-user settings, but existing approaches cannot adapt to a specific user without retraining, and do not account for intermittent contacts during locomotion. We propose ExoLaN, a Context-Aware DeLaN for human-exoskeleton interaction that learns the full coupled system dynamics while adapting to changes in interaction context. ExoLaN combines temporal context with partial contact-force measurements from force-sensitive insoles to infer latent dynamics embeddings and estimate generalized contact torques. On seven unseen users performing 21 unseen tasks, ExoLaN reduces torque estimation MSE by 7% compared to a black-box baseline. Beyond inverse dynamics, ExoLaN serves as a unified model that also enables accurate forward prediction: training with a multi-step prediction loss reduces acceleration MSE by 59% and long-horizon position and velocity errors by 60% and 93%, respectively, compared with a single-step loss. Moreover, the learned latent context captures task information without explicit task labels, making it a promising signal for task-aware assistive control.

</details>


### [164] [Learning to Leverage Compliance: A Policy-Admittance Learning Framework for Robotic Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31439)
*Chongren Wang,Minghe Li,Honghua Dai,Zhicheng Lin,Shiyang Wei,Xiaokui Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出LeCo（Leverage Compliance）策略-导纳学习框架，通过多速率反馈将高频接触交互记录聚合为策略转移奖励，并用冲突代价与方向性高力尾部代价抑制"策略持续加载/控制器卸载"的低效接触，在四个真实接插件装配任务上取得94%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姿态误差与接触不确定性下，策略学习与柔顺控制虽各自有效，但直接结合并不保证协调：策略可能持续施压而导纳控制器退让，形成持续加载却进展有限的僵持状态，需要显式建模并惩罚这种策略与控制器之间的"对抗"。

Method: 提出LeCo策略-导纳学习框架：固定导纳参数下由策略输出引导视觉策略执行；采用多速率反馈机制将高频接触交互记录聚合为策略转移层级的奖励；引入集成冲突代价刻画持续性的"策略加载/控制器卸载"对抗，并以方向性高力尾部代价捕捉单次转移内的持续加载事件；与任务完成奖励共同构成复合奖励，通过强化学习优化视觉策略。

Result: 在四个真实连接器装配任务上总体成功率达94%；相较基线，成功试验的平均合力峰值与力矩峰值分别下降约30%和64%；奖励消融显示加入冲突塑形后，成功试验的接触条件冲突密度中位数下降约53%。

Conclusion: 将多速率策略-导纳交互转化为互补的奖励信号，可使策略学会利用固定柔顺性，在降低接触载荷的同时实现有效插入，为接触丰富装配中的策略-控制器协同提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Policy learning and compliant control offer a promising route to reliable autonomous assembly under pose errors and contact uncertainty. However, combining them does not ensure coordination: the policy may continue pushing against contact while the controller yields, producing sustained loading with limited progress. To address this problem, we propose LeCo (Leverage Compliance), a policy-admittance learning framework that guides a visual policy through execution-time interaction under fixed admittance. A multirate feedback mechanism aggregates high-rate contact-interaction records into policy-transition rewards. An integrated conflict cost then characterizes sustained policy-loading/controller-unloading opposition, while a directional high-force tail cost captures continued-loading events within a transition. Together with task completion, these costs encourage the policy to leverage compliance with less unproductive loading. We evaluate LeCo on four real connector-assembly tasks, obtaining an aggregate success rate of 94%. Across tasks, mean successful-trial resultant-force and torque peaks decrease by approximately 30% and 64% relative to the comparison baseline. Reward ablation further shows that adding conflict shaping reduces median successful-trial contact-conditioned conflict density by approximately 53%. These results support learning to leverage fixed compliance by turning multirate policy-admittance interaction into complementary reward signals for effective, lower-load insertion.

</details>


### [165] [Vision-Based 6-DoF Grasp Pose Estimation for Robot Cloth Unfolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31452)
*Domen Tabernik,Peter Nimac,Jan Jerićević,Danijel Skočaj,Andrej Gams*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CeDiRNet-6DoF深度学习框架，通过双臂机器人在空中重新抓取策略实现布料展开，联合预测最优抓取点与完整6-DoF抓取位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布料具有可变形、高维特性，交互动力学复杂，且褶皱、边缘、抓取点等关键视觉线索常被遮挡导致感知歧义，布料展开成为挑战性任务。

Method: 采用"空中重抓取"双臂策略：一臂持布，另一臂在最优选点抓取以展开。提出CeDiRNet-6DoF，将稠密3D抓取回归与分割、正余弦编码欧拉角相融合，从观测到的布料构型中联合预测有效抓取点及完整6-DoF抓取位姿，以最大化展开面积为目标。

Result: 在ICRA 2024布料竞赛框架下的双臂机器人平台上广泛评估，达到state-of-the-art性能；消融实验验证了联合分割、背景随机化与图像裁剪等关键设计组件的有效性。

Conclusion: CeDiRNet-6DoF为无结构环境下可靠的机器人布料操作提供了稳健且通用的基础。

Abstract: Cloth manipulation is a challenging task due to the deformable and high-dimensional nature of cloth, which leads to complex interaction dynamics and perceptual ambiguity arising from frequent occlusions of critical visual cues such as folds, edges, and grasp points. In this work, we tackle cloth unfolding using a regrasping-in-the-air strategy, where one manipulator holds the cloth while the other grasps it at an optimally selected point to unfold it. To this end, we propose CeDiRNet-6DoF, a deep learning framework that jointly predicts effective grasp points and the complete 6-DoF grasp pose from the observed cloth configuration. By integrating dense 3D grasp regression with segmentation and sine-cosine-encoded Euler angles, the proposed method reliably estimates the grasp configuration that maximizes the unfolded cloth area. We extensively evaluated CeDiRNet-6DoF on a bimanual robotic setup within the ICRA 2024 Cloth Competition framework, achieving state-of-the-art performance. An ablation study further validates the benefits of key design components, including joint segmentation, background randomization, and image cropping. These results establish CeDiRNet-6DoF as a robust and versatile foundation for reliable robotic cloth manipulation in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [166] [Generate, Track, Improve: Perceptive Multi-Skill Humanoid Locomotion with RL-Fine-Tuned Motion Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31577)
*Zachary Olkin,William D. Compton,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两层人形机器人运动架构：感知式 flow matching 运动生成器从原始深度图规划全身轨迹，控制引导 RL 训练的感知跟踪策略执行；核心贡献是离策略 RL 微调循环（结构化搜索+优势加权回归）改进生成器，实现多技能、室外动态地形穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用人形机器人需要多技能、感知型、动态且鲁棒的运动控制器，能在任意人类可通行地形行走；现有方法或依赖里程计/高度图，或技能单一、地形一致性差，难以泛化到未见几何与技能组合。

Method: 两层架构：(1) 感知式 flow matching 运动生成器，输入原始深度图直接规划全身轨迹；(2) 感知跟踪策略，用 control-guided RL 训练以跟随生成的运动。两者在基于动态优化人体数据构建的地形一致运动片段库上训练。核心为离策略 RL 微调循环：用结构化搜索（structured search）与生成器交互收集数据，再做优势加权回归（AWR）更新生成器。

Result: 离策略微调比在策略残差微调样本效率高得多，并提升未见几何与技能组合下的地形一致性；成功地形穿越率最高提升 25 个百分点，技能选择提升最高 80 个百分点。仅用原始深度图，无需里程计或高度图，室外部署简便；双相机可提前感知地形并自适应调整速度。单一策略对使 Unitree G1 能行走、奔跑、站立、上下箱子跳跃及室外上下楼梯。

Conclusion: 深度图感知 + 生成-跟踪分层 + 离策略 RL 微调的组合，可在无里程计/高度图条件下实现单策略多技能室外动态地形运动，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: General purpose humanoids require locomotion controllers that are multi-skill, perceptive, dynamic, and robust enough to go anywhere humans can. In this work, we present a two layer locomotion architecture: (1) a perceptive flow matching motion generator plans whole body trajectories from raw depth images while a (2) perceptive tracking policy trained with control-guided RL follows these motions. Both policies are trained on a library of terrain consistent motion clips created with dynamically optimized human data which yields both accurate velocity tracking and terrain consistent references. Our central contribution is a simple yet effective off-policy RL fine tuning loop that improves the motion generator. A structured search method is used with the generator to gather data for advantage weighted regression. This off-policy loop is much more sample efficient than on-policy residual fine tuning and improves terrain consistency on unseen geometries and skill compositions. We find that successful terrain traversals increased by up to 25 percentage points and skill selection improved by up to 80 percentage points. By using raw depth images to perceive the environment no odometry or height maps are needed, and outdoor deployment is easy. With two cameras, the policy can see terrain coming from further away and adjust its velocity regardless of the commanded speed so it can traverse the terrain. A single policy pair enables a Unitree G1 humanoid to walk, run, stand, jump on and off of boxes, and traverse stairs in outdoor environments. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [167] [Learning Robot Policies from Sparse Success Signals via STL-Guided Stein Variational Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31606)
*Hongrui Zheng,Cristian Ioan Vasile,Antonio Loquercio,Rahul Mangharam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STL-SVPG：用平滑STL鲁棒度作为轨迹级目标，结合Stein变分策略梯度做群体式策略优化，在六个四旋翼/机械臂任务中五个取得最高成功率，并实现真实世界迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏成功信号下的机器人策略学习困难：任务完成往往依赖动作协同、精确接触结果或多个条件同时满足，且复杂物理交互加剧难度。传统奖励塑形虽提供稠密反馈，但局部进展未必转化为最终任务完成。

Method: 提出Signal Temporal Logic-guided Stein Variational Policy Gradient（STL-SVPG）：以平滑STL鲁棒度（robustness）作为轨迹级训练目标，通过动力学对该目标求导，将信用按动作对完整任务规范的影响分配，而非仅依据局部进展；算法为群体式（population-based）策略梯度方法。

Result: 在六个四旋翼与机械臂任务上评估，涵盖事件触发响应、严格有序行为、带截止时间的响应以及与物理世界的交互；STL-SVPG在六个基准中的五个上取得最高平均成功率；仿真训练的策略可将时序与接触类任务行为迁移到真实世界。

Conclusion: STL引导的轨迹级目标配合群体式策略梯度，为稀疏、时序约束与接触密集型机器人任务提供了有效且可迁移的学习范式。

Abstract: Learning robot policies for tasks with sparse success signals is challenging when completion depends on coordinated actions, precise contact outcomes, or satisfying several conditions together. Intricate physical interactions with the world further complicate these requirements. Prior work using conventional reward shaping mechanisms provides dense feedback but local progress might not translate into eventual task completion. We present Signal Temporal Logic-guided Stein Variational Policy Gradient (STL-SVPG), a population-based method that uses smooth STL robustness as a trajectory-level training objective. Differentiating this objective through the dynamics assigns credit to policy actions according to their effect on the complete task specification, rather than local progress alone. We evaluate the approach on six quadcopter and manipulator tasks that involves event-triggered responses, strictly ordered behavior, responses within specified deadlines, and physical interaction with the world. STL-SVPG achieves the highest mean success rate among the compared methods on five of six benchmarks. Simulation-trained policies trained in simulation transfer temporal and contact task behavior to the real world.

</details>
